from django.contrib import admin
from .models import Association

@admin.register(Association)
class AssociationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('name', 'president_name', 'establishment_date', 'created_at', 'created_by')
    list_filter = ('establishment_date', 'created_at')
    search_fields = ('name', 'president_name', 'address', 'email')
    date_hierarchy = 'created_at'
    readonly_fields = ('created_at', 'updated_at', 'created_by')
    fieldsets = (
        ('ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط£ط³ط§ط³ظٹط©', {
            'fields': ('name', 'establishment_date', 'president_name')
        }),
        ('ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط§طھطµط§ظ„', {
            'fields': ('address', 'phone', 'email')
        }),
        ('ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط¥ط¶ط§ظپظٹط©', {
            'fields': ('description',)
        }),
        ('ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…', {
            'fields': ('created_at', 'updated_at', 'created_by'),
            'classes': ('collapse',)
        }),
    )

    def save_model(self, request, obj, form, change):
        if not change:  # If creating a new object
            obj.created_by = request.user
        super().save_model(request, obj, form, change)

class MemberForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = Member
        fields = [
            # Personal information
            'name', 'surname', 'birth_date', 'birth_place', 'father_name',
            'mother_name', 'gender', 'nationality', 'id_number', 'id_issue_date',
            'address',

            # Contact information
            'phone', 'email',

            # Membership information
            'membership_type', 'position', 'custom_position',

            # Family status
            'marital_status',

            # Education and professional information
            'education_level', 'professional_status',

            # Status information
            'join_date', 'status', 'replacement_member',

            # Additional information
            'notes'
        ]
        widgets = {
            'birth_date': forms.DateInput(attrs={'type': 'date'}),
            'id_issue_date': forms.DateInput(attrs={'type': 'date'}),
            'join_date': forms.DateInput(attrs={'type': 'date'}),
            'address': forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 2}),
            'notes': forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 3}),
            'phone': forms.TextInput(attrs={'maxlength': '10', 'pattern': '[0-9]{10}', 'title': 'ظٹط¬ط¨ ط£ظ† ظٹطھظƒظˆظ† ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ ظ…ظ† 10 ط£ط±ظ‚ط§ظ… ظپظ‚ط·'}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.association = kwargs.pop('association', None)
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    def clean_phone(self):
        phone = self.cleaned_data.get('phone')
        if phone and len(phone) > 10:
            raise forms.ValidationError(_('ظٹط¬ط¨ ط£ظ† ظ„ط§ ظٹط²ظٹط¯ ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ ط¹ظ† 10 ط£ط±ظ‚ط§ظ…'))
        return phone

    def clean(self):
        cleaned_data = super().clean()
        name = cleaned_data.get('name')
        surname = cleaned_data.get('surname')
        id_number = cleaned_data.get('id_number')
        position = cleaned_data.get('position')
        birth_date = cleaned_data.get('birth_date')
        birth_place = cleaned_data.get('birth_place')

        # Skip validation if essential fields are missing
        if not (name and surname and id_number):
            return cleaned_data

        # Get current association
        current_association = self.association
        if not current_association:
            return cleaned_data

        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط¹ط¯ظ… طھظƒط±ط§ط± ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµ ظپظٹ ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        same_person_in_association = Member.objects.filter(
            association=current_association,
            name=name,
            surname=surname,
            id_number=id_number
        )

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ظٹظ„ط§ط¯ ظˆظ…ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظٹظ„ط§ط¯ ظ…طھظˆظپط±ظٹظ†طŒ ط£ط¶ظپظ‡ظ…ط§ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚
        if birth_date and birth_place:
            same_person_in_association = same_person_in_association.filter(
                birth_date=birth_date,
                birth_place=birth_place
            )

        # ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            same_person_in_association = same_person_in_association.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµ ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¨ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        if same_person_in_association.exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError(
                _('ظ‡ط°ط§ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµ ظ…ط³ط¬ظ„ ط¨ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ ظ‡ط°ظ‡ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©')
            )

        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط¹ط¯ظ… طھظƒط±ط§ط± ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ط§طµط¨ ط§ظ„ظ‚ظٹط§ط¯ظٹط©
        if position and position != 'member':
            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط¹ط¯ظ… ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط´ط®طµ ط¢ط®ط± ط¨ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨ ظپظٹ ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
            existing_position = Member.objects.filter(
                association=current_association,
                position=position
            )

            # ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«
            if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
                existing_position = existing_position.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)

            if existing_position.exists():
                position_display = dict(Member.POSITION_CHOICES)[position]
                raise forms.ValidationError(
                    _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط£ظƒط«ط± ظ…ظ† ط´ط®طµ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپظٹ ظ…ظ†طµط¨ \'%(position)s\'!'),
                    params={'position': position_display}
                )

        # Find existing memberships for this person in other associations
        existing_members = Member.objects.filter(
            name=name,
            surname=surname,
            id_number=id_number
        ).exclude(association=current_association)

        # Exclude current instance if updating
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            existing_members = existing_members.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)

        # Check if current association is sports type
        is_current_sports = current_association and current_association.association_type == 'sports'

        # Check if person is already in a sports association
        sports_memberships = existing_members.filter(association__association_type='sports')
        is_in_sports = sports_memberships.exists()

        # Get all associations where the person is a member
        association_names = [member.association.name for member in existing_members]

        # Rule 1: If trying to join a sports association but already in another association
        if is_current_sports and existing_members.exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError(
                _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ظ„ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§ظ„ط§ظ†ط¶ظ…ط§ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط© ظ„ط£ظ†ظ‡ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¨ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ: %(associations)s'),
                params={'associations': 'طŒ '.join(association_names)}
            )

        # Rule 2: If already in a sports association, cannot join any other
        if is_in_sports:
            sports_names = [member.association.name for member in sports_memberships]
            raise forms.ValidationError(
                _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ظ„ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§ظ„ط§ظ†ط¶ظ…ط§ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط£ظٹ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط£ط®ط±ظ‰ ظ„ط£ظ†ظ‡ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¨ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط©: %(associations)s'),
                params={'associations': 'طŒ '.join(sports_names)}
            )

        # Rule 3: Cannot be in more than 2 associations total
        if existing_members.count() >= 2:
            raise forms.ValidationError(
                _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ظ„ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§ظ„ط§ظ†ط¶ظ…ط§ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط£ظƒط«ط± ظ…ظ† ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹطھظٹظ†. ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ…ط³ط¬ظ„ ط¨ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ: %(associations)s'),
                params={'associations': 'طŒ '.join(association_names)}
            )

        # If we get here, the validation passes
        return cleaned_data

# ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ظ„ظ„ظˆطµظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ط³ظ…ظٹط©
class AssociationReceiptForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = AssociationReceipt
        fields = [
            'receipt_type', 'receipt_number', 'receipt_date', 'receipt_description'
        ]
        widgets = {
            'receipt_date': forms.DateInput(attrs={'type': 'date'}),
            'receipt_description': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.association = kwargs.pop('association', None)
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    def save(self, commit=True):
        instance = super().save(commit=False)
        if self.association:
            instance.association = self.association
        if commit:
            instance.save()
        return instance

from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta, date

class AssociationManager(models.Manager):
    def active(self):
        return self.filter(is_deleted=False)

    def deleted(self):
        return self.filter(is_deleted=True)

    current_tenure = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, choices=MANDATE_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©'))

    def __str__(self):

    def calculate_tenure_end_date(self):
        """
        ط­ط³ط§ط¨ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©:
        - 4 ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹط©
        - 3 ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط©
        - ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ط§ظ„ظƒط¨ظٹط³ط© ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ طµط­ظٹط­
        """
        if not self.tenure_start_date:
            return None

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¨ظ†ط§ط،ظ‹ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        if self.association_type == 'sports':
            years_to_add = 3
        else:
            years_to_add = 4

        # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ…ط¹ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ط§ظ„ظƒط¨ظٹط³ط©
        end_year = self.tenure_start_date.year + years_to_add
        try:
            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط­ظپط§ط¸ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ظٹظˆظ…/ط§ظ„ط´ظ‡ط±
            return date(end_year, self.tenure_start_date.month, self.tenure_start_date.day)
        except ValueError:
            # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط؛ظٹط± طµط§ظ„ط­ (ظ…ط«ظ„ 29 ظپط¨ط±ط§ظٹط± ظپظٹ ط³ظ†ط© ط؛ظٹط± ظƒط¨ظٹط³ط©)
            # ظ†ط¹ظˆط¯ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط¢ط®ط± ظٹظˆظ… ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط´ظ‡ط±
            return date(end_year, self.tenure_start_date.month + 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¹ظ†ط¯ طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨ط¯ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
        if self.tenure_start_date and not self.tenure_end_date:
            self.tenure_end_date = self.calculate_tenure_end_date()

        # ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھظپط¹ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھظ…ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط©
        if self.association_type == 'sports' and self.has_exceptional_extension and self.tenure_end_date:
            # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط³ظ†ط© ط¥ط¶ط§ظپظٹط© ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
            self.tenure_end_date = date(self.tenure_end_date.year + 1, self.tenure_end_date.month, self.tenure_end_date.day)

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ†طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط© ظ‡ظٹ ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³ ظˆظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨ط¯ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
        if self.status == 'establishment' and not self.tenure_start_date and self.establishment_date:
            self.tenure_start_date = self.establishment_date
            self.tenure_end_date = self.calculate_tenure_end_date()

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ط±ط© ط£ط®ط±ظ‰ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طھظ…ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط©
            if self.association_type == 'sports' and self.has_exceptional_extension and self.tenure_end_date:
                self.tenure_end_date = date(self.tenure_end_date.year + 1, self.tenure_end_date.month, self.tenure_end_date.day)

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ†طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط© ظ‡ظٹ ط§ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
        if self.status == 'renewal' and not self.tenure_start_date:
            self.tenure_start_date = date.today()
            self.tenure_end_date = self.calculate_tenure_end_date()
            self.current_tenure += 1

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ط±ط© ط£ط®ط±ظ‰ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طھظ…ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ظٹط§ط¶ظٹط©
            if self.association_type == 'sports' and self.has_exceptional_extension and self.tenure_end_date:
                self.tenure_end_date = date(self.tenure_end_date.year + 1, self.tenure_end_date.month, self.tenure_end_date.day)

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def get_tenure_status(self):
        if getattr(self, 'status', None) == 'dissolution':
            return 'ظ…ظˆظ‚ظˆظپط© ط¨ط³ط¨ط¨ ط­ظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'
        today = timezone.now().date() if hasattr(timezone, 'now') else date.today()
        if self.tenure_start_date and self.tenure_end_date:
            if self.tenure_start_date > today:
                return 'ظ„ظ… طھط¨ط¯ط£'
            elif self.tenure_end_date < today:
                return 'ظ…ظ†طھظ‡ظٹط©'
            else:
                return 'ط³ط§ط±ظٹط©'
        return 'ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯'

    def get_remaining_days(self):
        """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©"""
        if not self.tenure_end_date:
            return None

        today = date.today()
        return (self.tenure_end_date - today).days

    def can_renew_tenure(self):
        """ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط¥ظ…ظƒط§ظ†ظٹط© طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©"""
        # ظٹظ…ظƒظ† طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¯ط§ط¦ظ…ظ‹ط§ ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©
        return True

    def get_tenure_duration(self):
        """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¨ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ"""
        if self.association_type == 'sports':
            base_duration = 3
            if self.has_exceptional_extension:
                return f"{base_duration} ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ + ط³ظ†ط© طھظ…ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط¦ظٹ"
            return f"{base_duration} ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ"
        else:
            return "4 ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ"

    def get_tenure_duration_in_years(self):
        """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¨ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ظƒط±ظ‚ظ…"""
        if self.association_type == 'sports':
            return 4 if self.has_exceptional_extension else 3
        else:
            return 4

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ')
        unique_together = ('association', 'id_number')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.full_name} - {self.get_role_display()} ({self.association.name})"

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«'), auto_now=True)

    # Add a position_order field
    position_order = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(_('طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨'), default=10, editable=False)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # Set position_order based on position before saving
        position_order_map = {
            'president': 1,
            'vice_president': 2,
            'assistant1': 3,
            'secretary': 4,
            'vice_secretary': 5,
            'treasurer': 6,
            'vice_treasurer': 7,
            'assistant2': 8,
            'assistant3': 9,
            'assistant4': 10,
            'member': 11,
            'other': 12,
        }
        self.position_order = position_order_map.get(self.position, 99)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ط¹ط¶ظˆ')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،')
        ordering = ['position_order', 'name', 'surname']  # Use the actual field
        # Add constraints to prevent duplicate members
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=['name', 'surname', 'id_number'],
                name='unique_member_identity'
            )
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} {self.surname}"

    def get_full_position(self):
        if self.position == 'other' and self.custom_position:
            return self.custom_position
        return self.get_position_display()

# ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ط³ظ…ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
class AssociationReceipt(models.Model):
    RECEIPT_TYPES = [
        ('establishment', _('ظˆطµظ„ ط¥ظٹط¯ط§ط¹ ظ…ظ„ظپ طھط£ط³ظٹط³ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©')),
        ('registration', _('ظˆطµظ„ طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©')),
        ('reports', _('ظˆطµظ„ ط¥ظٹط¯ط§ط¹ ط§ظ„طھظ‚ط§ط±ظٹط± ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ظˆظٹط©')),
        ('modifications', _('ظˆطµظ„ ط¥ظٹط¯ط§ط¹ ط§ظ„طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ط§طھ')),
        ('dissolution', _('ظˆطµظ„ ط­ظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©')),
        ('other', _('ظˆطµظ„ ط¢ط®ط±')),
    ]

    association = models.ForeignKey(Association, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='receipts', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'))
    receipt_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=RECEIPT_TYPES, verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„'))
    receipt_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„'))
    receipt_date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„'))
    receipt_description = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('ظˆطµظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط،'))
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ظˆطµظ„ ط±ط³ظ…ظٹ')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط±ط³ظ…ظٹط©')
        ordering = ['-receipt_date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_receipt_type_display()} - {self.receipt_number} ({self.receipt_date})"

    def get_receipt_type_display(self):
        """
        ط¯ط§ظ„ط© ظ…ط®طµطµط© ظ„ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ†طµ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„
        """
        for code, text in self.RECEIPT_TYPES:
            if code == self.receipt_type:
                return text
        return self.receipt_type

# ط§ظ„طھط£ظƒط¯ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط¯ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„طھط±ط¬ظ…ط© ظ…طھط§ط­ط© ظپظٹ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط£ظ†ط­ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط­ط¯ط©

# ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
@login_required
def association_create(request):
    """
    ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط© ظˆظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ط© ط¨ظ‡ط§.
    ظٹطھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طµط­ط© ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬طŒ ظˆظٹظ…ظ†ط¹ طھظƒط±ط§ط± ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھطŒ ظˆظٹط¹ط±ط¶ ط±ط³ط§ط¦ظ„ ظ…ظ†ط§ط³ط¨ط©.
    """
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = AssociationForm(request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            try:
                # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط£ظˆظ„ط§ظ‹ ظ…ط¹ طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط±ظ‚ظ… طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ظپط±ظٹط¯
                association = form.save(commit=False)



                # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† created_by ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط§ظ‹
                if not association.created_by:
                    association.created_by = request.user

                # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط¹ ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                association.save()

                # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¥ط°ط§ ظˆط¬ط¯طھ
                if 'all_receipts' in request.POST and request.POST['all_receipts']:
                    try:
                        # طھط­ظ…ظٹظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ…ظ† JSON
                        receipts_data = json.loads(request.POST['all_receipts'])
                        logger.info(f"طھظ… ط§ط³طھظ„ط§ظ… ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: {len(receipts_data)}")

                        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ‚ط§ظ…ظˆط³ ظ„ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظپط±ظٹط¯ط©
                        unique_receipts = {}

                        # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ظƒظ„ ظˆطµظ„ ظˆط¥ط²ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„طھظƒط±ط§ط±
                        for receipt_data in receipts_data:
                            if not all(k in receipt_data for k in ['type', 'number', 'date']):
                                logger.info(f"ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ظƒطھظ…ظ„ط©: {receipt_data}")
                                continue

                            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ…ظپطھط§ط­ ظپط±ظٹط¯ ظ„ظƒظ„ ظˆطµظ„
                            key = f"{receipt_data['type']}_{receipt_data['number']}"
                            unique_receipts[key] = receipt_data

                        # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظپط±ظٹط¯ط© ظپظ‚ط·
                        for receipt_data in unique_receipts.values():
                            try:
                                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                                receipt = AssociationReceipt(
                                    association=association,
                                    receipt_type=receipt_data['type'],
                                    receipt_number=receipt_data['number'],
                                    receipt_date=receipt_data['date'],
                                    receipt_description=receipt_data.get('description', '')
                                )
                                receipt.save()
                                logger.info(f"طھظ… ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„: {receipt.pk} - {receipt.receipt_type} - {receipt.receipt_number}")
                            except Exception as save_error:
                                logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط§ظ„ظپط±ط¯ظٹ: {save_error}")
                    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                        logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ظپظƒ طھط±ظ…ظٹط² JSON: {e}")
                        logger.error(f"ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ„ظ…ط©: {request.POST['all_receipts']}")
                        messages.warning(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ'))
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: {e}")
                        messages.warning(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: ') + str(e))

                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
                return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association.pk)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ط¹ط§ظ… ظپظٹ ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {e}")
                messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: ') + str(e))
    else:
        form = AssociationForm()

    return render(request, 'associations/association_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'title': _('ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©'),
    })

@login_required
def association_update(request, pk):
    """
    طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط© ظˆظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ط© ط¨ظ‡ط§.
    ظٹطھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طµط­ط© ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬طŒ ظˆظٹظ…ظ†ط¹ طھظƒط±ط§ط± ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھطŒ ظˆظٹط¹ط±ط¶ ط±ط³ط§ط¦ظ„ ظ…ظ†ط§ط³ط¨ط©.
    """
    try:
        association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk)

        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ - ط§ظ„ط³ظ…ط§ط­ ظ„ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¬ظ„ظٹظ† ط¨ط§ظ„ط¯ط®ظˆظ„
        # ظ†ط²ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ظٹط¯ط© ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظƒط§ظ†طھ طھط³ط¨ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ط´ظƒظ„ط©

        if request.method == 'POST':
            form = AssociationForm(request.POST, instance=association)
            if form.is_valid():
                # ط­ظپط¸ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط¹ طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† created_by ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظپط§ط±ط؛ط§ظ‹
                association = form.save(commit=False)
                if not association.created_by:
                    association.created_by = request.user
                association.save()

                # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
                try:
                    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
                    if 'all_receipts' in request.POST and request.POST['all_receipts']:
                        try:
                            receipts_data = json.loads(request.POST['all_receipts'])

                            if receipts_data:
                                receipts_to_save = []
                                for receipt_data in receipts_data:
                                    if all(k in receipt_data for k in ['type', 'number', 'date']):
                                        # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®
                                        date_str = receipt_data['date']
                                        date_obj = None

                                        try:
                                            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ISO
                                            date_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
                                        except ValueError:
                                            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ط§طھ ط£ط®ط±ظ‰
                                            date_formats = ['%d/%m/%Y', '%m/%d/%Y', '%d-%m-%Y', '%m-%d-%Y']
                                            for date_format in date_formats:
                                                try:
                                                    date_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, date_format).date()
                                                    break
                                                except ValueError:
                                                    continue

                                        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®طŒ ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ
                                        if date_obj is None:
                                            date_obj = datetime.datetime.now().date()

                                        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                                        receipt = AssociationReceipt(
                                            association=association,
                                            receipt_type=receipt_data['type'],
                                            receipt_number=receipt_data['number'],
                                            receipt_date=date_obj,
                                            receipt_description=receipt_data.get('description', '')
                                        )
                                        receipts_to_save.append(receipt)

                                # ط­ظپط¸ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¯ظپط¹ط© ظˆط§ط­ط¯ط©
                                if receipts_to_save:
                                    AssociationReceipt.objects.bulk_create(receipts_to_save)
                        except json.JSONDecodeError:
                            messages.error(request, _('ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ. ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط¥ط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط¥ط¯ط®ط§ظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ طµط­ظٹط­.'))
                except Exception as e:
                    messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: ') + str(e))

                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
                return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association.pk)
            else:
                # ط¹ط±ط¶ ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬
                error_messages = []
                for field, errors in form.errors.items():
                    for error in errors:
                        error_messages.append(f"{form.fields[field].label if field in form.fields else field}: {error}")
                messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط§ظ„طھط§ظ„ظٹط©: ') + "<br>" + "<br>".join(error_messages))
        else:
            form = AssociationForm(instance=association)

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©
        receipts = AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(association=association).order_by('-receipt_date')

        return render(request, 'associations/association_form.html', {
            'form': form,
            'association': association,
            'receipts': receipts,
            'title': _('طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'),
        })
    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {e}")
        logger.error(traceback.format_exc())
        messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط؛ظٹط± ظ…طھظˆظ‚ط¹: ') + str(e))
        return redirect('associations:list')

@login_required
def association_detail(request, pk):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظˆظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھظ‡ط§
    """
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط£ظˆ ط¥ط¸ظ‡ط§ط± ط®ط·ط£ 404 ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… طھظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط©
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    members = Member.objects.filter(association=association)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط±طھط¨ط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® (ط§ظ„ط£ط­ط¯ط« ط£ظˆظ„ط§ظ‹)
    receipts = AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(association=association).order_by('-receipt_date')

    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ…ط§ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ†طھ ظ‡ظ†ط§ظƒ ط¥ظٹطµط§ظ„ط§طھ
    has_receipts = receipts.exists()

    # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ظ‚ظˆط§ظ…ظٹط³ ط¨ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ط© ط¢ظ…ظ†ط©
    receipts_data = []
    try:
        for receipt in receipts:
            receipts_data.append({
                'id': receipt.pk,
                'receipt_type': receipt.receipt_type,
                'receipt_number': receipt.receipt_number,
                'receipt_date': receipt.receipt_date.isoformat() if receipt.receipt_date else None,
                'receipt_description': receipt.receipt_description or '',
                'amount': str(receipt.amount) if hasattr(receipt, 'amount') and receipt.amount else '0',
            })
        logger.info(f"طھظ… طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ {len(receipts_data)} ظˆطµظ„ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: {str(e)}")
        receipts_data = []  # ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظپط§ط±ط؛ط© ظƒط¨ط¯ظٹظ„

    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
    logger.info(f"ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {pk} - ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: {receipts.count()}")

    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
    for receipt in receipts:
        logger.info(f"ظˆطµظ„: ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ={receipt.pk}, ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt.receipt_type}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt.receipt_number}, ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®={receipt.receipt_date}")

    # ط¹ط±ط¶ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
    return render(request, 'associations/association_detail.html', {
        'association': association,
        'members': members,
        'receipts': receipts,  # ط§ظ„ط§ط­طھظپط§ط¸ ط¨ظ€ QuerySet ط§ظ„ط£طµظ„ظٹ ظ„ظ„ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ظپظٹ ط£ظ…ط§ظƒظ† ط£ط®ط±ظ‰
        'receipts_data': receipts_data,  # ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ظˆظ„ط© ظ„ظ„ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ظپظٹ JavaScript
        'has_receipts': has_receipts
    })

@login_required
def association_list(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ…ط¹ ط®ظٹط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظˆط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    """
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظˆط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    search_query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    association_type = request.GET.get('type', '')
    status = request.GET.get('status', '')

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط´ط·ط© ظپظ‚ط· (ط؛ظٹط± ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط©)
    associations = Association.objects.active()

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹
    if association_type:
        associations = associations.filter(association_type=association_type)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
    if status:
        associations = associations.filter(status=status)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط§ظ„ظ…طھظ‚ط¯ظ…
    if search_query:
        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„طھط·ط§ط¨ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھط§ظ… (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ط¹ط§ظ„ظٹط©)
        exact_matches = Association.objects.filter(
            Q(name__iexact=search_query) |
            Q(approval_number__iexact=search_query)
        )

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ط­ط±ظˆظپ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظ‰ (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ظ…طھظˆط³ط·ط©)
        starts_with_matches = Association.objects.filter(
            Q(name__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(approval_number__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(president_name__istartswith=search_query)
        ).exclude(id__in=exact_matches.values_list('id', flat=True))

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ط§ط­طھظˆط§ط، (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ظ…ظ†ط®ظپط¶ط©)
        contains_matches = Association.objects.filter(
            Q(name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(approval_number__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(president_name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(address__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(goals__icontains=search_query)
        ).exclude(
            id__in=exact_matches.values_list('id', flat=True) |
            starts_with_matches.values_list('id', flat=True)
        )

        # ط¯ظ…ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط­ظپط§ط¸ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط©
        from itertools import chain
        associations = list(chain(exact_matches, starts_with_matches, contains_matches))

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ†طھ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬ ظپط§ط±ط؛ط©طŒ ط¬ط±ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ظƒظ„ظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ظپطµظ„ط©
        if not associations and ' ' in search_query:
            words = search_query.split()
            word_query = Q()
            for word in words:
                word_query |= Q(name__icontains=word) | Q(president_name__icontains=word)

            associations = Association.objects.filter(word_query)

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ظ„ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    if hasattr(associations, 'annotate'):
        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظ„ظ„طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ (QuerySet)
        associations = associations.annotate(
            unread_notifications=Count(
                'notifications',
                filter=Q(notifications__is_read=False),
                distinct=True
            ),
            read_notifications=Count(
                'notifications',
                filter=Q(notifications__is_read=True),
                distinct=True
            ),
            urgent_notifications=Count(
                'notifications',
                filter=Q(
                    notifications__is_read=False,
                    notifications__notification_type__in=[
                        'tenure_expiry_very_soon', 'tenure_expired', 'warning'
                    ]
                ),
                distinct=True
            )
        )
    else:
        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© (list)طŒ ظ†ط­طھط§ط¬ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط­ط¯ط©
        association_ids = [a.id for a in associations]

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط؛ظٹط± ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط© ظ„ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        unread_counts = {
            a_id: count for a_id, count in
            Notification.objects.filter(
                association_id__in=association_ids,
                is_read=False
            ).values('association_id').annotate(
                count=Count('id')
            ).values_list('association_id', 'count')
        }

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط© ظ„ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        read_counts = {
            a_id: count for a_id, count in
            Notification.objects.filter(
                association_id__in=association_ids,
                is_read=True
            ).values('association_id').annotate(
                count=Count('id')
            ).values_list('association_id', 'count')
        }

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¬ظ„ط© ظ„ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        urgent_counts = {
            a_id: count for a_id, count in
            Notification.objects.filter(
                association_id__in=association_ids,
                is_read=False,
                notification_type__in=['tenure_expiry_very_soon', 'tenure_expired', 'warning']
            ).values('association_id').annotate(
                count=Count('id')
            ).values_list('association_id', 'count')
        }

        # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظƒط§ط¦ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        for association in associations:
            association.unread_notifications = unread_counts.get(association.id, 0)
            association.read_notifications = read_counts.get(association.id, 0)
            association.urgent_notifications = urgent_counts.get(association.id, 0)

    # Pagination
    page = request.GET.get('page', 1)
    # طھط£ظƒط¯ ظ…ظ† طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظ‘ط­ ظ„طھط¬ظ†ظ‘ط¨ UnorderedObjectListWarning
    if hasattr(associations, 'order_by'):
        # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³ (ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط£ظ‚ط¯ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ط­ط¯ط«) ط«ظ… ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط© (ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظ‰ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ظٹط±ط©)
        associations = associations.order_by('establishment_date', 'current_tenure')
    else:
        # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظٹط¯ظˆظٹط§ظ‹
        associations.sort(key=lambda a: (a.establishment_date, a.current_tenure))
    paginator = Paginator(associations, 10)  # Show 10 associations per page

    try:
        associations = paginator.page(page)
    except PageNotAnInteger:
        associations = paginator.page(1)
    except EmptyPage:
        associations = paginator.page(paginator.num_pages)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ط§ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    association_types = Association.ASSOCIATION_TYPES

    # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط´ط·ط© (ط؛ظٹط± ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط©)
    all_active_associations = Association.objects.active()

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط§طھ
    total_registered = all_active_associations.count()
    establishment_count = all_active_associations.filter(status='establishment').count()
    renewal_count = all_active_associations.filter(status='renewal').count()
    suspension_count = all_active_associations.filter(status='suspension').count()
    warning_count = all_active_associations.filter(status='warning').count()
    dissolution_count = all_active_associations.filter(status='dissolution').count()

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط©
    deleted_count = Association.objects.deleted().count()

    # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط·ظ„ظˆط¨ط© ظ„ظ„ظ€ template
    # ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط´ط·ط©: ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³ ظˆط§ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
    active_count = establishment_count + renewal_count
    # ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ‚ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©: ط§ظ„طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ظˆط§ظ„ط¥ط¹ط°ط§ط±
    pending_count = suspension_count + warning_count

    return render(request, 'associations/association_list.html', {
        'associations': associations,
        'association_types': association_types,
        'current_filters': {
            'q': search_query,
            'type': association_type,
            'status': status,
        },
        # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ
        'total_registered': total_registered,
        'establishment_count': establishment_count,
        'renewal_count': renewal_count,
        'suspension_count': suspension_count,
        'warning_count': warning_count,
        'dissolution_count': dissolution_count,
        'deleted_count': deleted_count,
        # ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط·ظ„ظˆط¨ط© ظ„ظ„ظ€ template
        'active_count': active_count,
        'pending_count': pending_count,
    })

@login_required
@permission_required('associations.delete_association', raise_exception=True)
def association_delete(request, pk):
    """
    ط­ط°ظپ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© (طھط¹ظٹظٹظ†ظ‡ط§ ظƒظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط© ظˆظ„ظٹط³ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ).
    ظٹط³ظ…ط­ ظپظ‚ط· ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…طµط±ط­ ظ„ظ‡ظ….
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk)
    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط§ظ„ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظ†ط§ط¹ظ… (ظ†ظ‚ظ„ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط³ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‡ظ…ظ„ط§طھ)
            association.is_deleted = True
            association.deleted_at = timezone.now()
            association.deleted_by = request.user
            association.deletion_reason = request.POST.get('deletion_reason', '')
            association.save()

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ظ†ظ‚ظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط³ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‡ظ…ظ„ط§طھ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {}').format(str(e)))
            return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association.pk)

        return redirect('associations:list')

    return render(request, 'associations/association_confirm_delete.html', {
        'association': association
    })

# ظ†ظ…ط§ط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ

    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ طµظپط­ط© ظ†ظ…ط§ط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ
    """
    return render(request, 'associations/executive_board_templates.html')

@login_required
@xframe_options_exempt
def executive_board_template(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…
    """
    return render(request, 'associations/executive_board_template.html')

@login_required
@xframe_options_exempt
def executive_board_editable(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ظ„ظ„طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„
    """
    association_id = request.GET.get('association_id')
    context = {}

    if association_id:
        try:
            association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)
            members = Member.objects.filter(association=association).order_by('position_order')

            # طھط­ط¶ظٹط± ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ط§طµط¨
            executive_members = {
                'president': None,       # ط±ط¦ظٹط³ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
                'vice_president': None,  # ط§ظ„ظ†ط§ط¦ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„
                'assistant1': None,      # ط§ظ„ظ†ط§ط¦ط¨ ط§ظ„ط«ط§ظ†ظٹ
                'secretary': None,       # ط§ظ„ط£ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…
                'vice_secretary': None,  # ظ…ط³ط§ط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…
                'treasurer': None,       # ط£ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط§ظ„
                'vice_treasurer': None,  # ظ…ط³ط§ط¹ط¯ ط£ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط§ظ„
                'assistant2': None,      # ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط§ط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ظˆظ„
                'assistant3': None,      # ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط§ط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ط«ط§ظ†ظٹ
                'assistant4': None,      # ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط§ط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ط«ط§ظ„ط«
            }

            # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط­ط³ط¨ ظ…ظ†ط§طµط¨ظ‡ظ…
            for member in members:
                if member.position in executive_members:
                    executive_members[member.position] = member

            context = {
                'association': association,
                'members': members,
                'executive_members': executive_members
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط¬ظ„ط¨ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {e}")

    return render(request, 'associations/executive_board_editable.html', context)

@login_required
def deleted_associations(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط©
    """
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط© ظپظ‚ط·
    associations = Association.objects.deleted()

    # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظˆط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    search_query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    association_type = request.GET.get('type', '')

    if search_query:
        associations = associations.filter(
            Q(name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(approval_number__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(president_name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(deletion_reason__icontains=search_query)
        )

    if association_type:
        associations = associations.filter(association_type=association_type)

    # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط© ط­ط³ط¨ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³ (ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط£ظ‚ط¯ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ط­ط¯ط«) ط«ظ… ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط© (ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظ‰ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ظٹط±ط©)
    associations = associations.order_by('establishment_date', 'current_tenure')

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ط§ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    association_types = Association.ASSOCIATION_TYPES

    return render(request, 'associations/deleted_associations.html', {
        'associations': associations,
        'association_types': association_types,
        'current_filters': {
            'q': search_query,
            'type': association_type,
        }
    })

@login_required
@permission_required('associations.restore_association', raise_exception=True)
def restore_association(request, pk):
    """
    ط§ط³طھط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط­ط°ظˆظپط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk, is_deleted=True)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط§ط³طھط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
            association.is_deleted = False
            association.deleted_at = None
            association.deleted_by = None
            association.deletion_reason = None
            association.save()

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ…طھ ط§ط³طھط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            return redirect('associations:deleted_list')
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط§ط³طھط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©: {}').format(str(e)))

    return render(request, 'associations/association_confirm_restore.html', {'association': association})

@login_required
@permission_required('associations.permanent_delete_association', raise_exception=True)
def permanent_delete_association(request, pk):
    """
    ط­ط°ظپ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹط§ظ‹
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk, is_deleted=True)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ظٹظ† ط¨ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¨ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ط© ط¢ظ…ظ†ط©
            from .models import Member
            Member.objects.filter(association=association).delete()

            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ط© ط¨ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¥ظ† ظˆط¬ط¯طھ
            try:
                from django.apps import apps
                Document = apps.get_model('documents', 'Document')
                Document.objects.filter(association=association).delete()
            except Exception:
                # طھط¬ط§ظ‡ظ„ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ‡ظ†ط§ظƒ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
                pass

            # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ†ظپط³ظ‡ط§
            association.delete()

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹط§ظ‹ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            return redirect('associations:deleted_list')
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹط§ظ‹: {}').format(str(e)))

    return render(request, 'associations/association_confirm_permanent_delete.html', {'association': association})

# ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
@login_required
def member_list(request, association_id):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط¹ ط®ط§طµظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط«
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظˆط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    search_query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    status_filter = request.GET.get('status', '')
    position_filter = request.GET.get('position', '')

    # Get all members for this association
    members = Member.objects.filter(association=association)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
    if status_filter:
        members = members.filter(status=status_filter)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨
    if position_filter:
        members = members.filter(position=position_filter)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط§ظ„ظ…طھظ‚ط¯ظ…
    if search_query:
        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„طھط·ط§ط¨ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھط§ظ… (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ط¹ط§ظ„ظٹط©)
        exact_matches = members.filter(
            Q(name__iexact=search_query) |
            Q(surname__iexact=search_query) |
            Q(id_number__iexact=search_query) |
            Q(phone__iexact=search_query)
        )

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ط­ط±ظˆظپ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظ‰ (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ظ…طھظˆط³ط·ط©)
        starts_with_matches = members.filter(
            Q(name__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(surname__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(id_number__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(phone__istartswith=search_query) |
            Q(custom_position__istartswith=search_query)
        ).exclude(id__in=exact_matches.values_list('id', flat=True))

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ط§ط­طھظˆط§ط، (ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط© ظ…ظ†ط®ظپط¶ط©)
        contains_matches = members.filter(
            Q(name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(surname__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(id_number__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(phone__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(email__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(address__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(custom_position__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(notes__icontains=search_query)
        ).exclude(
            id__in=exact_matches.values_list('id', flat=True) |
            starts_with_matches.values_list('id', flat=True)
        )

        # ط¯ظ…ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط­ظپط§ط¸ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ظˆظٹط©
        from itertools import chain
        members = list(chain(exact_matches, starts_with_matches, contains_matches))

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ†طھ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬ ظپط§ط±ط؛ط©طŒ ط¬ط±ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¨ط§ظ„ظƒظ„ظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ظپطµظ„ط©
        if not members and ' ' in search_query:
            words = search_query.split()
            word_query = Q()
            for word in words:
                word_query |= (
                    Q(name__icontains=word) |
                    Q(surname__icontains=word) |
                    Q(custom_position__icontains=word)
                )
            members = Member.objects.filter(association=association).filter(word_query)

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
    total_members = Member.objects.filter(association=association).count()
    active_members = Member.objects.filter(association=association, status='active').count()
    frozen_members = Member.objects.filter(association=association, status='frozen').count()
    resigned_members = Member.objects.filter(association=association, status='resigned').count()
    deceased_members = Member.objects.filter(association=association, status='deceased').count()

    # ط®ظٹط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    status_choices = Member.STATUS_CHOICES
    position_choices = Member.POSITION_CHOICES

    return render(request, 'associations/member_list.html', {
        'association': association,
        'members': members,
        'search_query': search_query,
        'status_filter': status_filter,
        'position_filter': position_filter,
        'total_members': total_members,
        'active_members': active_members,
        'frozen_members': frozen_members,
        'resigned_members': resigned_members,
        'deceased_members': deceased_members,
        'status_choices': status_choices,
        'position_choices': position_choices,
    })

@login_required
def member_create(request, association_id):
    """
    ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = MemberForm(request.POST, association=association)
        if form.is_valid():
            member = form.save(commit=False)
            member.association = association
            member.save()

            # طھط®طµظٹطµ ط±ط³ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ†ط¬ط§ط­ ط¨ظ†ط§ط،ظ‹ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨
            if member.position == 'member':
                messages.success(request, _('طھظ…طھ ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            else:
                position_display = dict(Member.POSITION_CHOICES)[member.position]
                messages.success(request, _('طھظ…طھ ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© %(position)s ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.') % {'position': position_display})
            return redirect('associations:member_list', association_id=association.id)
        else:
            # Add this to ensure form errors are displayed
            messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط£ط¯ظ†ط§ظ‡.'))
    else:
        form = MemberForm(association=association)

    return render(request, 'associations/member_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'association': association
    })

@login_required
def member_update(request, association_id, member_id):
    """
    طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)
    member = get_object_or_404(Member, pk=member_id, association=association)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = MemberForm(request.POST, instance=member, association=association)
        if form.is_valid():
            member = form.save()

            # طھط®طµظٹطµ ط±ط³ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ†ط¬ط§ط­ ط¨ظ†ط§ط،ظ‹ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨
            if member.position == 'member':
                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            else:
                position_display = dict(Member.POSITION_CHOICES)[member.position]
                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ %(position)s ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.') % {'position': position_display})
            return redirect('associations:member_list', association_id=association.id)
        else:
            # Add this to ensure form errors are displayed
            messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط£ط¯ظ†ط§ظ‡.'))
    else:
        form = MemberForm(instance=member, association=association)

    return render(request, 'associations/member_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'association': association,
        'member': member
    })

@login_required
def member_delete(request, association_id, member_id):
    """
    ط­ط°ظپ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)
    member = get_object_or_404(Member, pk=member_id, association=association)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        member.delete()
        messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
        return redirect('associations:member_list', association_id=association.id)

    return render(request, 'associations/member_confirm_delete.html', {
        'association': association,
        'member': member
    })

    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    """
