    })

# ط§ظ„ط¥ظٹطµط§ظ„ط§طھ
@login_required
def update_association_receipts(request, association_id):
    """
    طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ظ† ط®ظ„ط§ظ„ ط§ظ„ظ†ط§ظپط°ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ط¨ط«ظ‚ط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
            if 'all_receipts' in request.POST and request.POST['all_receipts']:
                try:
                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ JSON ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ„ظ…ط© ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
                    raw_json = request.POST['all_receipts']
                    logger.info(f"ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ JSON ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ„ظ…ط©: {raw_json}")

                    # طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ…ظ† JSON
                    receipts_data = json.loads(raw_json)

                    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©
                    existing_receipts = list(AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(association=association))

                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
                    logger.info(f"ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.id}: {len(existing_receipts)}")
                    for receipt in existing_receipts:
                        logger.info(f"ظˆطµظ„ ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ: ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ={receipt.pk}, ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt.receipt_type}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt.receipt_number}")

                    # ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظ„طھط®ط²ظٹظ† ظ…ط¹ط±ظپط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھظٹ طھظ…طھ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬طھظ‡ط§
                    processed_receipt_ids = []

                    # ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظ„ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط© ط§ظ„طھظٹ ط³ظٹطھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپطھظ‡ط§
                    receipts_to_create = []

                    # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ظƒظ„ ظˆطµظ„ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط³ظ„ط©
                    if receipts_data:
                        for receipt_data in receipts_data:
                            if all(k in receipt_data for k in ['type', 'number', 'date']):
                                # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®
                                date_str = receipt_data['date']
                                date_obj = None

                                try:
                                    # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ISO
                                    date_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
                                except ValueError:
                                    # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ط§طھ ط£ط®ط±ظ‰
                                    date_formats = ['%d/%m/%Y', '%m/%d/%Y', '%d-%m-%Y', '%m-%d-%Y']
                                    for date_format in date_formats:
                                        try:
                                            date_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_str, date_format).date()
                                            break
                                        except ValueError:
                                            continue

                                # ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®طŒ ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ
                                if date_obj is None:
                                    date_obj = datetime.datetime.now().date()

                                # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¹ظ† ظˆطµظ„ ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¨ظ†ظپط³ ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…
                                existing_receipt = None
                                for receipt in existing_receipts:
                                    if receipt.receipt_type == receipt_data['type'] and receipt.receipt_number == receipt_data['number']:
                                        existing_receipt = receipt
                                        break

                                if existing_receipt:
                                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
                                    logger.info(f"طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظˆطµظ„ ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯: ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ={existing_receipt.pk}, ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt_data['type']}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt_data['number']}")

                                    # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯
                                    existing_receipt.receipt_date = date_obj
                                    existing_receipt.receipt_description = receipt_data.get('description', '')
                                    existing_receipt.save()

                                    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©
                                    processed_receipt_ids.append(existing_receipt.id)
                                else:
                                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
                                    logger.info(f"ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯: ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt_data['type']}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt_data['number']}")

                                    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                                    new_receipt = AssociationReceipt(
                                        association=association,
                                        receipt_type=receipt_data['type'],
                                        receipt_number=receipt_data['number'],
                                        receipt_date=date_obj,
                                        receipt_description=receipt_data.get('description', '')
                                    )
                                    receipts_to_create.append(new_receipt)

                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط¹ظ† ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھظٹ طھظ…طھ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬طھظ‡ط§
                    logger.info(f"ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھظٹ طھظ…طھ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬طھظ‡ط§: {len(processed_receipt_ids)}")
                    logger.info(f"ظ…ط¹ط±ظپط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھظٹ طھظ…طھ ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬طھظ‡ط§: {processed_receipt_ids}")

                    # ظ„ط§ ظ†ظ‚ظˆظ… ط¨ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ…ط© ط£ط¨ط¯ط§ظ‹
                    # ظ†ظ‚ظˆظ… ظپظ‚ط· ط¨طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط© ط£ظˆ ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
                    logger.info(f"ظ„ظ† ظٹطھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط£ظٹ ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ…ط© ظˆظپظ‚ط§ظ‹ ظ„ظ„ط³ظٹط§ط³ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©")

                    # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط¹ظ† ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
                    logger.info(f"ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط© ط§ظ„طھظٹ ط³ظٹطھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط¤ظ‡ط§: {len(receipts_to_create)}")
                    for i, receipt in enumerate(receipts_to_create):
                        logger.info(f"ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ {i+1}: ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt.receipt_type}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt.receipt_number}")

                    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط© ط¯ظپط¹ط© ظˆط§ط­ط¯ط©
                    if receipts_to_create:
                        AssociationReceipt.objects.bulk_create(receipts_to_create)

                    # ط¹ط±ط¶ ط±ط³ط§ظ„ط© ظ†ط¬ط§ط­
                    messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))

                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    messages.error(request, _('ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ. ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط¥ط¹ط§ط¯ط© ط¥ط¯ط®ط§ظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ طµط­ظٹط­.'))
            else:
                # ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ط£ظٹ ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھطŒ ظ„ط§ ظ†ظ‚ظˆظ… ط¨ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©
                # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ظ„ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­
                logger.info(f"ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ط£ظٹ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ظ„ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.id}")
                messages.info(request, _('ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… ط¥ط¬ط±ط§ط، ط£ظٹ طھط؛ظٹظٹط±ط§طھ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ'))
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ: ') + str(e))

        return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association_id)

    # ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ط§ظ„ط·ظ„ط¨ POSTطŒ ظ‚ظ… ط¨ط§ظ„طھظˆط¬ظٹظ‡ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ طµظپط­ط© طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association_id)

@login_required
def add_receipt(request, association_id):
    """
    ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    """
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            form = AssociationReceiptForm(request.POST)
            if form.is_valid():
                # Verificar si ya existe un recibo con el mismo tipo y nأ؛mero para esta asociaciأ³n
                receipt_type = form.cleaned_data['receipt_type']
                receipt_number = form.cleaned_data['receipt_number']

                existing_receipt = AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(
                    association=association,
                    receipt_type=receipt_type,
                    receipt_number=receipt_number
                ).first()

                if existing_receipt:
                    # Actualizar el recibo existente en lugar de crear uno nuevo
                    existing_receipt.receipt_date = form.cleaned_data['receipt_date']
                    existing_receipt.receipt_description = form.cleaned_data.get('receipt_description', '')
                    existing_receipt.save()
                    messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
                else:
                    # Crear un nuevo recibo
                    receipt = form.save(commit=False)
                    receipt.association = association
                    receipt.save()
                    messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))

                return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association_id)
            else:
                logger.info(f"Form errors: {form.errors}")
                messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬'))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error saving receipt: {e}")
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„: ') + str(e))
    else:
        form = AssociationReceiptForm()

    return render(request, 'associations/receipt_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'association': association,
        'title': _('ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆطµظ„ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'),
    })

@login_required
def delete_receipt(request, receipt_id):
    """
    ط­ط°ظپ ظˆطµظ„ ظ…ط¹ظٹظ† ظ…ط¹ طھط£ظƒظٹط¯
    """
    receipt = get_object_or_404(AssociationReceipt, pk=receipt_id)
    association_id = receipt.association.id

    # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط·ظ„ط¨ GETطŒ ط¹ط±ط¶ طµظپط­ط© ط§ظ„طھط£ظƒظٹط¯
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render(request, 'associations/receipt_confirm_delete.html', {
            'receipt': receipt,
            'association': receipt.association
        })

    # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط·ظ„ط¨ POSTطŒ ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„
    elif request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„طھطµط­ظٹط­ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط§ظ„ط­ط°ظپ
            logger.info(f"ط¬ط§ط±ظٹ ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„: ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ={receipt.pk}, ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹={receipt.receipt_type}, ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ…={receipt.receipt_number}")

            # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„
            receipt.delete()

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ…ط§ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ظ‚ط¯ طھظ… ط­ط°ظپظ‡ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ طµط­ظٹط­
            if AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(pk=receipt_id).exists():
                logger.info(f"ط®ط·ط£: ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ {receipt_id} ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ طµط­ظٹط­")
                messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„'))
            else:
                logger.info(f"طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ {receipt_id} ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­")
                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))

            # ط§ظ„ط±ط¬ظˆط¹ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
            return redirect('associations:receipts_list')
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ {receipt_id}: {e}")
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„: ') + str(e))
            return redirect('associations:receipts_list')

# ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
@login_required
def receipts_list(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ ظ…ط¹ ط¥ظ…ظƒط§ظ†ظٹط© ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±ط©
    """
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظˆط§ظ„طھطµظپظٹط©
    search_query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    association_id = request.GET.get('association_id')
    receipt_type = request.GET.get('type', '')

    # ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ط£ط³ط§ط³ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
    receipts = AssociationReceipt.objects.all().order_by('-receipt_date')

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ظپظ„طھط± ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    if association_id:
        receipts = receipts.filter(association_id=association_id)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ظپظ„طھط± ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„
    if receipt_type:
        receipts = receipts.filter(receipt_type=receipt_type)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط«
    if search_query:
        receipts = receipts.filter(
            Q(receipt_number__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(receipt_description__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(association__name__icontains=search_query)
        )

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ظ„ظپظ„طھط±ط©
    associations = Association.objects.filter(is_deleted=False).order_by('name')

    # طھظ‚ط³ظٹظ… ط§ظ„طµظپط­ط§طھ
    paginator = Paginator(receipts, 20)  # 20 ظˆطµظ„ ظپظٹ ظƒظ„ طµظپط­ط©
    page = request.GET.get('page')
    try:
        receipts = paginator.page(page)
    except PageNotAnInteger:
        receipts = paginator.page(1)
    except EmptyPage:
        receipts = paginator.page(paginator.num_pages)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
    receipt_types = AssociationReceipt.RECEIPT_TYPES

    return render(request, 'associations/receipts_list.html', {
        'receipts': receipts,
        'associations': associations,
        'receipt_types': receipt_types,
        'current_filters': {
            'q': search_query,
            'association_id': association_id,
            'type': receipt_type,
        },
        'title': _('ط¥ط¯ط§ط±ط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ')
    })

@login_required
def receipt_update(request, receipt_id):
    """
    طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظˆطµظ„ ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯
    """
    receipt = get_object_or_404(AssociationReceipt, pk=receipt_id)
    association = receipt.association

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = AssociationReceiptForm(request.POST, instance=receipt)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆطµظ„ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
            return redirect('associations:receipts_list')
        else:
            messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬'))
    else:
        form = AssociationReceiptForm(instance=receipt)

    return render(request, 'associations/receipt_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'association': association,
        'title': _('طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ ظˆطµظ„'),
        'receipt': receipt
    })

# ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ
@login_required
def comprehensive_statistics(request):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ط© ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
    """
    from django.db.models import Count, Avg
    from django.db.models.functions import TruncMonth
    import datetime

    # طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ†ط·ط§ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط® (ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹط§ظ‹: ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط©)
    current_year = datetime.datetime.now().year
    year = request.GET.get('year', current_year)
    try:
        year = int(year)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        year = current_year

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…طھط§ط­ط©
    years = Association.objects.dates('establishment_date', 'year')
    years = [d.year for d in years]
    if current_year not in years:
        years.append(current_year)
    years.sort(reverse=True)

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„طھطµظ†ظٹظپ
    type_stats = []
    for type_choice in Association.ASSOCIATION_TYPES:
        type_id = type_choice[0]
        type_name = type_choice[1]
        type_count = Association.objects.filter(association_type=type_id).count()
        if Association.objects.count() > 0:
            type_percentage = round((type_count / Association.objects.count()) * 100, 1)
        else:
            type_percentage = 0

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ط³ظ…ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظپظٹ ظ‡ط°ط§ ط§ظ„طھطµظ†ظٹظپ
        type_associations = Association.objects.filter(association_type=type_id).values_list('name', flat=True)

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط­ط³ط¨ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        if type_id == 'sports':
            tenure_duration = "3 ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ (ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ط© ظ„ظ„طھظ…ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط«ظ†ط§ط¦ظٹ)"
        else:
            tenure_duration = "4 ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ"

        type_stats.append({
            'type_id': type_id,
            'type_name': type_name,
            'count': type_count,
            'percentage': type_percentage,
            'tenure_duration': tenure_duration,
            'associations': list(type_associations)
        })

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
    status_stats = Association.objects.filter(
        created_at__year=year
    ).values('status').annotate(
        count=Count('id')
    ).order_by('status')

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط© ظˆط§ظ„ظ†ط³ط¨ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط¦ظˆظٹط©
    total_count = Association.objects.filter(created_at__year=year).count()

    for stat in status_stats:
        for status_code, status_name in Association.STATUS_CHOICES:
            if stat['status'] == status_code:
                stat['status_name'] = status_name
                break

        # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ†ط³ط¨ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط¦ظˆظٹط©
        if total_count > 0:
            stat['percentage'] = round((stat['count'] / total_count) * 100, 1)
        else:
            stat['percentage'] = 0

        # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ظƒظ„ ط­ط§ظ„ط©
        associations_with_status = Association.objects.filter(
            created_at__year=year,
            status=stat['status']
        ).values_list('name', flat=True)

        stat['associations'] = list(associations_with_status)
        stat['associations_list'] = ', '.join(associations_with_status)

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط´ظ‡ط±ظٹ ظ„ظ„ط³ظ†ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط©
    monthly_registrations = Association.objects.filter(
        establishment_date__year=year
    ).annotate(
        month=TruncMonth('establishment_date')
    ).values('month').annotate(
        count=Count('id')
    ).order_by('month')

    # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ظ…ظ†ط§ط³ط¨ ظ„ظ„ط±ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ظٹ
    months = []
    registration_counts = []

    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط¨ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ط£ط´ظ‡ط± ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ط©
    for i in range(1, 13):
        month_date = datetime.date(year, i, 1)
        month_name = month_date.strftime('%B')  # ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ط´ظ‡ط±
        months.append(month_name)

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ط¹ظ† ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ط§طھ ظپظٹ ظ‡ط°ط§ ط§ظ„ط´ظ‡ط±
        count = 0
        for reg in monthly_registrations:
            if reg['month'].month == i:
                count = reg['count']
                break
        registration_counts.append(count)

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، - ط£ط¹ظ„ظ‰ 10 ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
    top_associations = Association.objects.annotate(
        member_count=Count('association_members')
    ).order_by('-member_count')[:10]

    # ظ…طھظˆط³ط· ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ظ„ظƒظ„ طھطµظ†ظٹظپ
    avg_members_by_type = Association.objects.values('association_type').annotate(
        avg_members=Avg('members_count')
    ).order_by('association_type')

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„طھطµظ†ظٹظپ
    for stat in avg_members_by_type:
        for type_code, type_name in Association.ASSOCIATION_TYPES:
            if stat['association_type'] == type_code:
                stat['type_name'] = type_name
                break
        # طھظ‚ط±ظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…طھظˆط³ط· ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط±ظ‚ظ…ظٹظ† ط¹ط´ط±ظٹظٹظ†
        if stat['avg_members'] is not None:
            stat['avg_members'] = round(stat['avg_members'], 2)

    # ط¥ط¬ظ…ط§ظ„ظٹ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…
    total_members = Member.objects.count()

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط¥ط¶ط§ظپظٹط©
    # ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط§ظپط¸ط©/ط§ظ„ظˆظ„ط§ظٹط© (ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ…طھط§ط­ط§ظ‹)
    # ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط²ظٹط¯ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ط¬ط©

    # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط¥ط¬ظ…ط§ظ„ظٹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¬ظ„ط© ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط©
    yearly_total = Association.objects.filter(created_at__year=year).count()

    # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ QuerySet ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ظ‚ط¨ظ„ طھظ…ط±ظٹط±ظ‡ط§ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    status_stats_list = list(status_stats)

    return render(request, 'associations/comprehensive_statistics.html', {
        'title': _('ط§ظ„ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ط© ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ'),
        'type_stats': type_stats, # type_stats is already a list
        'status_stats': status_stats_list, # Pass the list instead of the QuerySet
        'total_associations': yearly_total,
        'total_members': total_members,
        'top_associations': top_associations, # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط£ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
        'years': years,
        'selected_year': year,
    })

@login_required
def export_data(request):
    """
    طھطµط¯ظٹط± ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ظ„ظپ Excel ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط§ط­طھط±ط§ظپظٹ ظˆط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظپطµظ„ط©
    """
    try:
        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ…طµظ†ظپ Excel ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ (طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط®ط·ط£ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ…ظ„ط§ط¦ظٹ)
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "طھطµط¯ظٹط± ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ"

        # ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط£ظ†ظ…ط§ط· ط§ظ„ط®ظ„ط§ظٹط§ (طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ظ„ظˆط§ظ†)
        header_font = Font(name='Calibri', bold=True, size=11, color='FFFFFF')
        header_fill = PatternFill(start_color='4361ee', end_color='3f37c9', fill_type='solid')
        border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin', color='000000'),
            right=Side(style='thin', color='000000'),
            top=Side(style='thin', color='000000'),
            bottom=Side(style='thin', color='000000')
        )
        center_alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center', vertical='center', wrap_text=True)
        right_alignment = Alignment(horizontal='right', vertical='center', wrap_text=True)

        # ط¹ظ†ط§ظˆظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ظ…ط¯ط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„طµظˆط±ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ظپظ‚ط©
        headers = [
            "ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„",
            "ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©",
            "ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©",
            "طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³",
            "طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯",
            "ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ",
            "ط§ظ„ط¨ط±ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ظƒطھط±ظˆظ†ظٹ",
            "ط§ظ„ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ†",
            "ط§ظ„ط±ط¦ظٹط³",
            "طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨ط¯ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©",
            "طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©",
            "طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„",
            "ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،"
        ]

        # ظƒطھط§ط¨ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ†ط§ظˆظٹظ† ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚
        for col_num, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=1, column=col_num, value=header)
            cell.font = header_font
            cell.fill = header_fill
            cell.border = border
            cell.alignment = center_alignment

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط´ط·ط© ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ط©
        associations = Association.objects.active().select_related().prefetch_related('members')

        # ظƒطھط§ط¨ط© ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
        for row_num, assoc in enumerate(associations, 2):
            # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
            members_count = assoc.members.count()

            # طھط­ط¶ظٹط± ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
            data = [
                assoc.approval_number or f"REG-{assoc.id:04d}",  # ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„
                assoc.get_association_type_display(),             # ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
                assoc.get_status_display(),                       # ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
                assoc.establishment_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if assoc.establishment_date else '',  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³
                assoc.tenure_start_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if assoc.tenure_start_date else '',    # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                getattr(assoc, 'phone', '') or '0777777777',     # ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ (ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ)
                getattr(assoc, 'email', '') or f"info{assoc.id}@example.com",  # ط§ظ„ط¨ط±ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ظƒطھط±ظˆظ†ظٹ (ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ)
                assoc.address or 'ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯',                     # ط§ظ„ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ†
                assoc.president_name or 'ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯',               # ط§ظ„ط±ط¦ظٹط³
                assoc.tenure_start_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if assoc.tenure_start_date else '',    # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨ط¯ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
                assoc.tenure_end_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if assoc.tenure_end_date else '',        # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
                assoc.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if assoc.created_at else '',                  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„
                members_count                                     # ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
            ]

            # ظƒطھط§ط¨ط© ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚
            for col_num, value in enumerate(data, 1):
                cell = ws.cell(row=row_num, column=col_num, value=value)
                cell.border = border

                # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط§ط°ط§ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†ط§ط³ط¨ط©
                if col_num in [1, 13]:  # ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ظˆط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
                    cell.alignment = center_alignment
                elif col_num in [4, 5, 10, 11, 12]:  # ط§ظ„طھظˆط§ط±ظٹط®
                    cell.alignment = center_alignment
                else:  # ط§ظ„ظ†طµظˆطµ
                    cell.alignment = right_alignment

                # طھظ„ظˆظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط®ظ„ط§ظٹط§ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط© (ط¹ظ…ظˆط¯ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©)
                if col_num == 3:  # ط¹ظ…ظˆط¯ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
                    status_value = data[2]  # ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
                    if status_value == 'طھط£ط³ظٹط³':
                        cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color='E3F2FD', end_color='E3F2FD', fill_type='solid')
                    elif status_value == 'طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯':
                        cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color='E8F5E8', end_color='E8F5E8', fill_type='solid')
                    elif status_value == 'طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚':
                        cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color='FFF3E0', end_color='FFF3E0', fill_type='solid')
                    elif status_value == 'ط¥ط¹ط°ط§ط±':
                        cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color='FFEBEE', end_color='FFEBEE', fill_type='solid')
                    elif status_value == 'ط­ظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©':
                        cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color='FFCDD2', end_color='FFCDD2', fill_type='solid')

        # ط¶ط¨ط· ط¹ط±ط¶ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ظ…ط¯ط© ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ط­طھظˆظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
        column_widths = {
            'A': 15,  # ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„
            'B': 18,  # ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
            'C': 12,  # ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
            'D': 12,  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط£ط³ظٹط³
            'E': 12,  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
            'F': 15,  # ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ
            'G': 25,  # ط§ظ„ط¨ط±ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ظƒطھط±ظˆظ†ظٹ
            'H': 25,  # ط§ظ„ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ†
            'I': 20,  # ط§ظ„ط±ط¦ظٹط³
            'J': 15,  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¨ط¯ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
            'K': 15,  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ†ظ‡ط§ظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
            'L': 12,  # طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„
            'M': 10   # ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
        }

        for column, width in column_widths.items():
            ws.column_dimensions[column].width = width

        # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ط´ط±ط·ظٹ ظ„ط¹ظ…ظˆط¯ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
        if len(associations) > 0:
            color_scale_rule = ColorScaleRule(
                start_type='min', start_color='FFCDD2',
                mid_type='percentile', mid_color='FFF9C4',
                end_type='max', end_color='C8E6C9'
            )
            ws.conditional_formatting.add(f"M2:M{len(associations)+1}", color_scale_rule)

        # طھط¬ظ…ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„طµظپ ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„
        ws.freeze_panes = 'A2'

        # ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¬ط§ط¨ط©
        response = HttpResponse(
            content_type='application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet'
        )
        response['Content-Disposition'] = 'attachment; filename="associations_detailed_export.xlsx"'

        # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
        wb.save(response)

        return response

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ طھطµط¯ظٹط± ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ: {str(e)}")
        return HttpResponse(
            f"ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط§ظ„طھطµط¯ظٹط±: {str(e)}",
            status=500,
            content_type='text/plain; charset=utf-8'
        )


@login_required
def association_statistics(request, pk):
    """
    ط¹ط±ط¶ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ طھظپطµظٹظ„ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط¹ظٹظ†ط©
    """
    from django.db.models import Count, Avg, Q
    from django.db.models.functions import TruncMonth
    from datetime import datetime, timedelta

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=pk)

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط،
    members = Member.objects.filter(association=association)
    total_members = members.count()
    active_members = members.filter(status='active').count()
    inactive_members = members.filter(status='inactive').count()

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹
    male_members = members.filter(gender='male').count()
    female_members = members.filter(gender='female').count()

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ظپط¦ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ…ط±ظٹط©
    today = datetime.now().date()
    youth_members = members.filter(birth_date__gte=today - timedelta(days=35*365)).count()  # ط£ظ‚ظ„ ظ…ظ† 35 ط³ظ†ط©
    middle_age_members = members.filter(
        birth_date__lt=today - timedelta(days=35*365),
        birth_date__gte=today - timedelta(days=55*365)
    ).count()  # 35-55 ط³ظ†ط©
    senior_members = members.filter(birth_date__lt=today - timedelta(days=55*365)).count()  # ط£ظƒط«ط± ظ…ظ† 55 ط³ظ†ط©

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ط§طھ
    receipts = AssociationReceipt.objects.filter(association=association)
    total_receipts = receipts.count()
    receipts_by_type = receipts.values('receipt_type').annotate(count=Count('id'))

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط´ظ‡ط±ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط£ط¹ط¶ط§ط، (ط¢ط®ط± 12 ط´ظ‡ط±)
    monthly_members = members.extra(
        select={'month': "strftime('%%Y-%%m', created_at)"}
    ).values('month').annotate(count=Count('id')).order_by('month')

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
    tenure_info = {
        'start_date': association.tenure_start_date,
        'end_date': association.tenure_end_date,
        'current_tenure': association.get_current_tenure_display(),
        'days_remaining': (association.tenure_end_date - today).days if association.tenure_end_date else None
    }

    context = {
        'association': association,
        'total_members': total_members,
        'active_members': active_members,
        'inactive_members': inactive_members,
        'male_members': male_members,
        'female_members': female_members,
        'youth_members': youth_members,
        'middle_age_members': middle_age_members,
        'senior_members': senior_members,
        'total_receipts': total_receipts,
        'receipts_by_type': receipts_by_type,
        'monthly_members': monthly_members,
        'tenure_info': tenure_info,
    }

    return render(request, 'associations/association_statistics.html', context)

from django import forms
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.utils import timezone
from .models import Document, DocumentType, DocumentComment, DocumentHistory
from apps.associations.models import Association
import datetime
import logging

class DocumentForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = Document
        fields = ['title', 'file', 'description', 'association', 'status', 'expiry_date', 'notes']
        widgets = {
            'description': forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 4}),
            'notes': forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 3}),
            'expiry_date': forms.DateInput(attrs={'type': 'date'}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.user = kwargs.pop('user', None)
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        # طھط­ط³ظٹظ† ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
        self.fields['file'].widget.attrs.update({'class': 'form-control-file'})
        self.fields['title'].widget.attrs.update({'class': 'form-control'})
        self.fields['description'].widget.attrs.update({'class': 'form-control'})
        self.fields['notes'].widget.attrs.update({'class': 'form-control'})

        # ط¬ط¹ظ„ ط­ظ‚ظ„ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط·ظ„ظˆط¨ ط¹ظ†ط¯ ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            self.fields['file'].required = False

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ظ„ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«طŒ ط§ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط³ط§ط¨ظ‚ط© ظ„ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طھط؛ظٹظٹط±
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            self.initial_status = self.instance.status

    def save(self, commit=True):
        instance = super().save(commit=False)

        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
        if hasattr(self, 'initial_status') and instance.status != self.initial_status:
            instance._status_changed = True

            # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ظˆط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹
            if instance.status in ['APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'NEEDS_REVISION']:
                instance.review_date = timezone.now()
                if self.user:
                    instance.reviewed_by = self.user

        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ…ظ„ظپ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬
        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        if 'file' in self.files:
            logger.debug(f"File in form: {self.files['file'].name}, {self.files['file'].size} bytes")
            # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
            file = self.files['file']
            instance.file_size = file.size
            instance.file_type = file.content_type
        else:
            logger.debug("No file in form")

        if commit:
            try:
                instance.save()
                logger.debug(f"Document saved successfully with ID: {instance.pk}")

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„طھط؛ظٹظٹط±
                if hasattr(self, 'initial_status') and instance.status != self.initial_status:
                    DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                        document=instance,
                        status=instance.status,
                        notes=instance.notes,
                        user=self.user
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error saving document: {str(e)}")
                raise

        return instance

        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
        required=False,
        help_text=_('ط³ظٹطھظ… ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯')
    )

    file = forms.FileField(
        label=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ'),
        widget=forms.FileInput(attrs={
            'class': 'custom-file-input',
            'accept': '.pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.txt'
        }),
        required=True,

    )

    status = forms.ChoiceField(
        label=_('ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
        choices=Document.STATUS_CHOICES,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
        required=True,
        error_messages={'required': _('ظ‡ط°ط§ ط§ظ„ط­ظ‚ظ„ ظ…ط·ظ„ظˆط¨.')}
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Document

        widgets = {
            'description': forms.Textarea(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'rows': 3}),
            'notes': forms.Textarea(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'rows': 2}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.user = kwargs.pop('user', None)
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ
        default_type = DocumentType.objects.filter(is_default=True).first()
        if default_type:
            self.fields['document_type'].initial = default_type

    def save(self, commit=True):
        instance = super().save(commit=False)
        if self.user:
            instance.uploaded_by = self.user

        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ظ„ظ„طھظˆط§ظپظ‚
        if not instance.document_type_old:
            instance.document_type_old = 'other'

        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ…ط³ط­ط§ظ‹ ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹط§ظ‹
        if getattr(instance, 'is_scanned', False):
            instance.scan_date = timezone.now()
            instance.status = 'PENDING'

        logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        logger.debug(f"Saving document: {instance.title}")

        if commit:
            instance.save()
            logger.debug(f"Document saved with ID: {instance.id}")
        return instance

from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.utils import timezone
from django.core.files.storage import FileSystemStorage
from django.conf import settings
import os
import uuid
import logging
from apps.associations.models import Association
from apps.notifications.models import Notification
import datetime
from .validators import FileSizeValidator, validate_file_extension
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
import base64
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile

# ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ظ†ط¸ط§ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# طھط®ط²ظٹظ† ظ…ط´ظپط± ظ„ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ
class EncryptedFileStorage(FileSystemStorage):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طھظپط¹ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ط§طھ
        self.encryption_enabled = getattr(settings, 'STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_ENABLED', False)
        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ظپطھط§ط­ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ط§طھ ط£ظˆ ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ظ…ظپطھط§ط­ ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ ظ„ظ„طھط·ظˆظٹط±
        self.encryption_key = getattr(settings, 'STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY', 'default_development_key_not_for_production')

    def _save(self, name, content):
        # طھط®ط²ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹ ط­ط§ظ„ظٹظ‹ط§ - ظٹظ…ظƒظ† طھظ†ظپظٹط° ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ‚ط¨ظ„
        # ط¹ظ†ط¯ظ…ط§ ظٹطھظ… طھط«ط¨ظٹطھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ„ط§ط²ظ…ط©
        if self.encryption_enabled:
            # ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط©: ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ظپط¹ظ„ ط­ط§ظ„ظٹظ‹ط§ - ظٹطھظ… ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ظƒظ…ط§ ظ‡ظˆ
            logger.warning("ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظ…ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ط§طھ ظˆظ„ظƒظ† ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ظ†ظپط° ط¨ط¹ط¯.")
            # ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ‚ط¨ظ„طŒ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظƒظˆط¯ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظ‡ظ†ط§
        return super()._save(name, content)

    def _open(self, name, mode='rb'):
        # ظپطھط­ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹ
        file_object = super()._open(name, mode)
        if self.encryption_enabled:
            # ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط©: ظپظƒ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ظپط¹ظ„ ط­ط§ظ„ظٹظ‹ط§ - ظٹطھظ… ط¥ط±ط¬ط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ظƒظ…ط§ ظ‡ظˆ
            logger.warning("ظپظƒ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظ…ظپط¹ظ„ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ط§طھ ظˆظ„ظƒظ† ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ظ†ظپط° ط¨ط¹ط¯.")
            # ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھظ‚ط¨ظ„طŒ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظƒظˆط¯ ظپظƒ ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط± ظ‡ظ†ط§
        return file_object

# ط¯ط§ظ„ط© ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ…ط³ط§ط± ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظˆط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®
def association_directory_path(instance, filename):
    # ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط³طھط­ظپط¸ ظپظٹ: MEDIA_ROOT/documents/association_<id>/<year>/<month>/<filename>
    today = timezone.now()
    ext = filename.split('.')[-1].lower()
    new_filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{ext}"

    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ…ط³ط§ط± ظ…ظ†ط¸ظ… ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظˆط§ظ„طھط§ط±ظٹط®
    return f'documents/association_{instance.association.id}/{today.year}/{today.month:02d}/{new_filename}'

class DocumentType(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ'))
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظˆطµظپ'))
    required = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('ط¥ظ„ط²ط§ظ…ظٹ'))
    processing_deadline = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=21, verbose_name=_('ظ…ظ‡ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© (ط¨ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ…)'))
    is_default = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ظ†ظˆط¹ ظ…ظ„ظپ')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ')

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ‡ط°ط§ ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ظƒط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹطŒ ظ‚ظ… ط¨ط¥ظ„ط؛ط§ط، طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط±ظ‰
        if self.is_default:
            DocumentType.objects.filter(is_default=True).update(is_default=False)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

class Document(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('PENDING', _('ظ‚ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©')),
        ('APPROVED', _('ظ…ظˆط§ظپظ‚ ط¹ظ„ظٹظ‡')),
        ('REJECTED', _('ظ…ط±ظپظˆط¶')),
        ('NEEDS_REVISION', _('ظٹط­طھط§ط¬ طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ط§طھ')),
        ('EXPIRED', _('ظ…ظ†طھظ‡ظٹ ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط©')),
    ]

    # ط§ظ„ط®ظٹط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ…ط© ظ„ظ„طھظˆط§ظپظ‚ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط©
    DOCUMENT_TYPES = (
        ('statute', _('ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ط£ط³ط§ط³ظٹ')),
        ('minutes', _('ظ…ط­ط¶ط± ط§ط¬طھظ…ط§ط¹')),
        ('financial', _('طھظ‚ط±ظٹط± ظ…ط§ظ„ظٹ')),
        ('activity', _('طھظ‚ط±ظٹط± ظ†ط´ط§ط·')),
        ('other', _('ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط£ط®ط±ظ‰')),
    )

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ط£ط³ط§ط³ظٹط©
    title = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'))
    document_type = models.ForeignKey(
        DocumentType,
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
        null=True,  # ظ„ظ„ط³ظ…ط§ط­ ط¨ط§ظ„طھظˆط§ظپظ‚ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ…ط©
    )
    # ط§ظ„ط­ظ‚ظ„ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ظ„ظ„طھظˆط§ظپظ‚ ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ط©
    document_type_old = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=DOCUMENT_TYPES,
        verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© (ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ…)'),
        null=True,
        blank=True
    )
    file = models.FileField(
        upload_to=association_directory_path,
        verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ'),
        storage=EncryptedFileStorage(),
        validators=[
            FileSizeValidator(10 * 1024 * 1024),  # 10 ظ…ظٹط؛ط§ط¨ط§ظٹطھ ظƒط­ط¯ ط£ظ‚طµظ‰
            validate_file_extension
        ],
        help_text=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ظ…ظˆط­ ط¨ظ‡ط§: PDF, Word, Excel, طµظˆط±. ط§ظ„ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ظ‚طµظ‰ ظ„ظ„ط­ط¬ظ…: 10 ظ…ظٹط؛ط§ط¨ط§ظٹطھ')
    )
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('ظˆطµظپ'))
    association = models.ForeignKey(Association, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='documents', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'))
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, related_name='uploaded_documents', verbose_name=_('طھظ… ط§ظ„ط±ظپط¹ ط¨ظˆط§ط³ط·ط©'))

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='PENDING', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©'))
    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='reviewed_documents', verbose_name=_('طھظ…طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط¨ظˆط§ط³ط·ط©'))
    review_date = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©'))
    expiry_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط©'))
    processing_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©'))
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط§طھ'))

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ
    is_scanned = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('طھظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ'))
    scan_date = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ'))
    scan_notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ'))

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
    file_size = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ط­ط¬ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ (ط¨ط§ظٹطھ)'))
    file_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ'))

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظٹظ†ط© ظˆط§ظ„ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط©
    last_viewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¢ط®ط± ظ…ط¹ط§ظٹظ†ط©'))
    last_printed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط¢ط®ط± ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط©'))
    view_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('ط¹ط¯ط¯ ظ…ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظٹظ†ط©'))
    print_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('ط¹ط¯ط¯ ظ…ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط©'))

    # ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ظ…ط§ظ†
    is_encrypted = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ط´ظپط±'))
    encryption_method = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='AES-256', verbose_name=_('ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„طھط´ظپظٹط±'))

    # ظ†ط¸ط§ظ… ط§ظ„طھط±ظ‚ظٹظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ظ„ط³ظ„ظٹ
    document_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'))

    # طھظˆط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط©'))
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.association.name}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
        if not self.document_type:
            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ
            default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.filter(is_default=True).first()
            if default_document_type:
                self.document_type = default_document_type

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ‡ظ†ط§ظƒ ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
        if self.file and hasattr(self.file, 'size') and not self.file_size:
            # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط­ط¬ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
            self.file_size = self.file.size

            # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
            if hasattr(self.file, 'content_type'):
                self.file_type = self.file.content_type

        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯
        if not self.processing_deadline and self.document_type and self.document_type.processing_deadline:
            today = timezone.now().date()
            self.processing_deadline = today + datetime.timedelta(days=self.document_type.processing_deadline)

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ
        if self.is_scanned and not self.scan_date:
            self.scan_date = timezone.now()

        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ظ„ظ„طھظˆط§ظپظ‚
        if not self.document_type_old:
            self.document_type_old = 'other'
            
        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط±ظ‚ظ… ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© طھط³ظ„ط³ظ„ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ظ‹ط§ ط£ظˆ ظ…ظپظ‚ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
        if not self.document_number:
            current_year = timezone.now().year
            last_doc = Document.objects.filter(
                created_at__year=current_year
            ).order_by('-document_number').first()
            
            if last_doc and last_doc.document_number:
                try:
                    # ط§ط³طھط®ط±ط§ط¬ ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ظ„ط³ظ„ظٹ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ DOC-YYYY-XXXX
                    # طھط£ظƒط¯ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط§ظ„ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„طھط³ظ„ط³ظ„ظٹ ظٹط­طھظˆظٹ ظپظ‚ط· ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ط±ظ‚ط§ظ…
                    last_num_str = ''.join(filter(str.isdigit, last_doc.document_number.split('-')[-1]))
                    last_num = int(last_num_str) if last_num_str else 0
                    next_num = last_num + 1
                except (IndexError, ValueError):
                    next_num = 1
            else:
                next_num = 1
                
            self.document_number = f"DOC-{current_year}-{next_num:04d}"

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def create_status_notification(self):
        """ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¹ظ†ط¯ طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
        status_display = dict(self.STATUS_CHOICES)[self.status]
        title = f"طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: {self.title}"
        message = f"طھظ… طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© '{self.title}' ط¥ظ„ظ‰ '{status_display}'"

        if self.status == 'NEEDS_REVISION':
            message += f". ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط§طھ: {self.notes}"

        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±
        Notification.objects.create(
            title=title,
            message=message,
            notification_type='document_status',
            association=self.association,
            expires_at=timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(days=30)
        )

    def check_deadline(self):
        """ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ‚طھط±ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©"""
        if self.status == 'PENDING' and self.processing_deadline:
            days_left = (self.processing_deadline - timezone.now().date()).days
            if days_left <= 2:
                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ„ظ„طھط°ظƒظٹط± ط¨ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ
                title = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© {self.title}"
                message = f"ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© '{self.title}' ط¨ط­ط§ط¬ط© ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©. ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ظ‡ظˆ {self.processing_deadline} (ط®ظ„ط§ظ„ {days_left} ظٹظˆظ…)."

                Notification.objects.create(
                    title=title,
                    message=message,
                    notification_type='document_deadline',
                    association=self.association,
                    expires_at=timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(days=3)
                )
                return True

    def record_view(self):
        """طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ظ…ط¹ط§ظٹظ†ط© ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
        self.view_count += 1
        self.last_viewed_at = timezone.now()
        self.save(update_fields=['view_count', 'last_viewed_at'])

    def record_print(self):
        """طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
        self.print_count += 1
        self.last_printed_at = timezone.now()
        self.save(update_fields=['print_count', 'last_printed_at'])

    def get_file_size_display(self):
        """ط¹ط±ط¶ ط­ط¬ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¨ط´ظƒظ„ ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،"""
        if not self.file_size:
            return _('ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط¹ط±ظˆظپ')

        # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط­ط¬ظ… ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظˆط­ط¯ط§طھ ظ…ظ†ط§ط³ط¨ط©
        size_bytes = self.file_size
        if size_bytes < 1024:
            return f"{size_bytes} ط¨ط§ظٹطھ"
        elif size_bytes < 1024 * 1024:
            return f"{size_bytes / 1024:.2f} ظƒظٹظ„ظˆط¨ط§ظٹطھ"
        else:
            return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.2f} ظ…ظٹط؛ط§ط¨ط§ظٹطھ"

    def get_deadline_date(self):
        """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©"""
        if self.processing_deadline:
            return self.processing_deadline
        elif self.document_type and self.document_type.processing_deadline:
            return self.created_at.date() + datetime.timedelta(days=self.document_type.processing_deadline)
        return None

    def get_remaining_days(self):
        """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ"""
        deadline = self.get_deadline_date()
        if deadline:
            return (deadline - timezone.now().date()).days
        return None

    @property
    def is_deadline_approaching(self):
        """ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ…ط§ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظٹظ‚طھط±ط¨ (ط£ظ‚ظ„ ظ…ظ† 5 ط£ظٹط§ظ…)"""
        days = self.get_remaining_days()
        return days is not None and 2 < days <= 5

    @property
    def is_deadline_close(self):
        """ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ…ط§ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ‚ط±ظٹط¨ ط¬ط¯ظ‹ط§ (ط£ظ‚ظ„ ظ…ظ† ط£ظˆ ظٹط³ط§ظˆظٹ 2 ظٹظˆظ…)"""
        days = self.get_remaining_days()
        return days is not None and days <= 2

class DocumentHistory(models.Model):
    """ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® طھط؛ظٹظٹط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = models.ForeignKey(Document, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='history', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'))
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Document.STATUS_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©'))
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ظ„ط§ط­ط¸ط§طھ'))
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, related_name='document_history', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط؛ظٹظٹط±'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط³ط¬ظ„ط§طھ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.document.title} - {self.get_status_display()} - {self.created_at}"

class DocumentComment(models.Model):
    """طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ط§طھ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚"""
    document = models.ForeignKey(Document, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='comments', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'))
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='document_comments', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'))
    comment = models.TextField(verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚'))
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to='document_comments/', null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ط±ظپظ‚'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط©'))
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„طھط­ط¯ظٹط«'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
        verbose_name_plural = _('طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ط§طھ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ {self.document.title} ط¨ظˆط§ط³ط·ط© {self.user.username}"

from django.urls import path
from . import views
from . import views_document_actions

app_name = 'documents'

urlpatterns = [
    # ظ…ط³ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    path('', views.document_list, name='list'),
    path('create/', views.document_create, name='create'),
    path('create-simple/', views.document_create_simple, name='create_simple'),
    path('scan/', views.scan_document, name='scan'),
    path('<int:pk>/', views.document_detail, name='detail'),
    path('<int:pk>/update/', views.document_update, name='update'),
    path('<int:pk>/delete/', views.document_delete, name='delete'),
    path('<int:pk>/review/', views.document_review, name='review'),
    path('<int:pk>/comment/', views.document_comment, name='comment'),
    path('<int:pk>/history/', views.document_history, name='history'),
    path('statistics/', views.statistics_view, name='statistics_view'),

    # ظ…ط³ط§ط±ط§طھ ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    path('types/', views.document_type_list, name='document_type_list'),
    path('types/create/', views.document_type_create, name='document_type_create'),
    path('types/<int:pk>/update/', views.document_type_update, name='document_type_update'),

    # ظ…ط³ط§ط±ط§طھ API
    path('api/scan-upload/', views.api_scan_upload, name='api_scan_upload'),
    path('api/check-deadlines/', views.api_check_deadlines, name='api_check_deadlines'),

    # ظ…ط³ط§ط±ط§طھ ظ…ط¹ط§ظٹظ†ط© ظˆط·ط¨ط§ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    path('<int:document_id>/preview/', views_document_actions.preview_document, name='preview_document'),
    path('<int:document_id>/print/', views_document_actions.print_document, name='print_document'),
    path('storage-info/', views_document_actions.document_storage_info, name='storage_info'),
    path('storage-info/<int:association_id>/', views_document_actions.document_storage_info, name='storage_info_association'),
    
    # ظ…ط³ط§ط± ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط©
    path('association/<int:association_id>/', views.document_list, name='association_documents'),
]

from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
