# ط¯ط§ظ„ط© ظ„ظ„طھط¹ط§ظ…ظ„ ظ…ط¹ ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، CSRF
def csrf_failure(request, reason=""):
    return render(request, 'csrf_error.html', {
        'reason': reason,
        'title': _('ط®ط·ط£ ظپظٹ ط±ظ…ط² CSRF')
    })

@login_required
def document_create_simple(request):
    """طµظپط­ط© ط¨ط³ظٹط·ط© ظ„ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©"""
    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط±ظ…ط² CSRF ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط³ظٹط§ظ‚
    from django.middleware.csrf import get_token
    csrf_token = get_token(request)
    logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    logger.debug(f"document_create_simple: method={request.method}, CSRF Token generated")

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
    associations = Association.objects.all()

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    from .utils import get_document_statistics
    document_stats = get_document_statistics()

    if request.method == 'POST':
        logger.debug(f"POST data: {request.POST}")
        logger.debug(f"FILES data: {request.FILES}")

        form = SimpleDocumentForm(request.POST, request.FILES, user=request.user)
        logger.debug(f"Form is bound: {form.is_bound}")
        logger.debug(f"Form errors before validation: {form.errors}")

        if form.is_valid():
            logger.debug("Form is valid")
            try:
                document = form.save(commit=False)
                document.uploaded_by = request.user

                # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
                if 'file' in request.FILES:
                    file = request.FILES['file']
                    document.file_size = file.size
                    document.file_type = file.content_type

                document.save()
                logger.debug(f"Document saved with ID: {document.id}")

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                    document=document,
                    status=document.status,
                    notes=_('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                    user=request.user
                )

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ظ†ظٹظٹظ†
                Notification.objects.create(
                    title=_('ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©'),
                    message=_('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©: {}').format(document.title),
                    related_object_id=document.id,
                    related_object_type='document',
                    user=request.user
                )

                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
                return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.id)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error saving document: {str(e)}")
                messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: {}').format(str(e)))
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Form is invalid. Errors: {form.errors}")
            for field, errors in form.errors.items():
                for error in errors:
                    messages.error(request, f"{field}: {error}")
    else:
        form = SimpleDocumentForm(user=request.user)

    return render(request, 'documents/document_create_simple.html', {
        'form': form,
        'associations': associations,
        'document_stats': document_stats,
        'title': _('ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©'),
        'csrf_token': csrf_token
    })

@login_required
def document_list(request, association_id=None):
    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±
    filter_form = DocumentFilterForm(request.GET)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    documents = Document.objects.select_related('association', 'document_type', 'uploaded_by').all()

    # ظپظ„طھط±ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط­ط³ط¨ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
    # ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¤ظˆظ„ظˆظ† ظٹظ…ظƒظ†ظ‡ظ… ط±ط¤ظٹط© ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚طŒ ط¨ظٹظ†ظ…ط§ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظˆظ† ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹظˆظ† ظٹط±ظˆظ† ظپظ‚ط· ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ظ‡ظ…
    if not request.user.is_staff:
        # ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظˆظ† ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹظˆظ† ظٹط±ظˆظ† ظپظ‚ط· ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ‚ط§ظ…ظˆط§ ط¨ط±ظپط¹ظ‡ط§
        documents = documents.filter(uploaded_by=request.user)

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±ط©
    # ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھظ…ط±ظٹط± ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط§ط±طŒ ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظ‡. ظˆط¥ظ„ط§طŒ ط§ط¨ط­ط« ط¹ظ†ظ‡ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ…
    if association_id is None:
        association_id = request.GET.get('association')
    status = request.GET.get('status')
    document_type_id = request.GET.get('document_type')
    search_query = request.GET.get('search')
    date_from = request.GET.get('date_from')
    date_to = request.GET.get('date_to')
    view_mode = request.GET.get('view_mode', 'list')  # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆط¶ط¹ ط§ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶: list ط£ظˆ grid

    # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹط© ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ط§ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    current_filters = {
        'association': association_id,
        'status': status,
        'document_type': document_type_id,
        'search': search_query,
        'date_from': date_from,
        'date_to': date_to,
        'view_mode': view_mode,
    }

    # طھط·ط¨ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±ط§طھ
    if association_id:
        documents = documents.filter(association_id=association_id)

    if status:
        documents = documents.filter(status=status)

    if document_type_id:
        documents = documents.filter(document_type_id=document_type_id)

    if date_from:
        try:
            date_from = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_from, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            documents = documents.filter(created_at__date__gte=date_from)
        except ValueError:
            pass

    if date_to:
        try:
            date_to = datetime.datetime.strptime(date_to, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            documents = documents.filter(created_at__date__lte=date_to)
        except ValueError:
            pass

    if search_query:
        documents = documents.filter(
            Q(title__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(description__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(association__name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(document_type__name__icontains=search_query) |
            Q(uploaded_by__username__icontains=search_query)
        )

    # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ظ†طھط§ط¦ط¬
    documents = documents.order_by('-created_at')

    # طھظ‚ط³ظٹظ… ط§ظ„طµظپط­ط§طھ ظ„ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ ط§ظ„طھظ‚ظ„ظٹط¯ظٹ
    paginator = Paginator(documents, 10)  # 10 ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظپظٹ ظƒظ„ طµظپط­ط©
    page_number = request.GET.get('page')
    page_obj = paginator.get_page(page_number)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ„ط¯ظٹظ‡ط§ ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
    # طھط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ ظپظٹ ظˆط¶ط¹ ط§ظ„ط´ط¨ظƒط©
    associations_with_docs = []

    if view_mode == 'grid':
        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظپط±ظٹط¯ط© ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„ظ…ط¹ط±ظˆط¶ط©
        association_ids = set(doc.association_id for doc in page_obj if doc.association)
        associations = Association.objects.filter(id__in=association_ids)

        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ‚ط§ظ…ظˆط³ ظ„ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ…ط¹ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚
        for association in associations:
            doc_count = sum(1 for doc in page_obj if doc.association_id == association.id)
            associations_with_docs.append({
                'id': association.id,
                'name': association.name,
                'doc_count': doc_count
            })

        # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط§ط³ظ…
        associations_with_docs.sort(key=lambda x: x['name'])

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ط§ ظپظٹ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±
    if request.user.is_staff:
        all_associations = Association.objects.all()
    else:
        # ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¯ظٹظٹظ†طŒ ط¹ط±ط¶ ظپظ‚ط· ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·ط© ط¨ظ‡ظ…
        all_associations = Association.objects.filter(id__in=Document.objects.filter(uploaded_by=request.user).values_list('association_id', flat=True).distinct())

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ط§ ظپظٹ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظپظ„طھط±
    document_types = DocumentType.objects.all()

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط®ظٹط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ط©
    status_choices = Document.STATUS_CHOICES

    return render(request, 'documents/document_list.html', {
        'page_obj': page_obj,
        'filter_form': filter_form,
        'associations': all_associations,
        'associations_with_docs': associations_with_docs,
        'document_types': document_types,
        'current_filters': current_filters,
        'status_choices': status_choices,
        'title': _('ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚')
    })

@login_required
def document_create(request):
    logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    logger.debug(f"document_create: method={request.method}, user={request.user}")

    if request.method == 'POST':
        logger.debug(f"POST data: {request.POST}")
        logger.debug(f"FILES data: {request.FILES}")

        form = DocumentForm(request.POST, request.FILES, user=request.user)
        if form.is_valid():
            try:
                document = form.save(commit=False)
                document.uploaded_by = request.user

                # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© طھظ„ظ‚ط§ط¦ظٹظ‹ط§ (ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ)
                default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.first()  # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ظˆظ„ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                if not default_document_type:
                    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
                    default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.create(
                        name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                        description=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                        required=False,
                        processing_deadline=30
                    )

                document.document_type = default_document_type
                document.save()

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                    document=document,
                    status=document.status,
                    notes=_('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                    user=request.user
                )

                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
                return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.pk)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error: {str(e)}")
                messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: ') + str(e))
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Form errors: {form.errors}")
    else:
        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط© ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط±ط§ط¨ط· (ط¥ظ† ظˆط¬ط¯طھ)
        association_id = request.GET.get('association')
        initial = {}
        if association_id:
            initial['association'] = association_id
        form = DocumentForm(initial=initial, user=request.user)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظ„ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    document_types = DocumentType.objects.all()

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط±ظ…ط² CSRF ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط³ظٹط§ظ‚
    from django.middleware.csrf import get_token
    csrf_token = get_token(request)
    logger.debug(f"CSRF Token generated for document_create")

    return render(request, 'documents/document_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'document_types': document_types,
        'csrf_token': csrf_token,
        'title': _('ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©')
    })

@login_required
def scan_document(request):
    """طµظپط­ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚"""
    if request.method == 'POST':
        if 'scan_data' in request.POST:
            # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ
            scan_data = request.POST.get('scan_data')
            logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
            logger.debug(f"Received scan data length: {len(scan_data) if scan_data else 0}")
            form = ScanDocumentForm(request.POST)

            if form.is_valid():
                try:
                    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
                    document = form.save(commit=False)
                    document.uploaded_by = request.user
                    document.is_scanned = True
                    document.scan_date = timezone.now()
                    document.status = 'PENDING'

                    # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© طھظ„ظ‚ط§ط¦ظٹظ‹ط§ (ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ)
                    default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.first()  # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ظˆظ„ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                    if not default_document_type:
                        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
                        default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.create(
                            name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                            description=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                            required=False,
                            processing_deadline=30
                        )

                    document.document_type = default_document_type

                    # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ base64 ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ظ„ظپ
                    try:
                        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
                        if isinstance(scan_data, str) and scan_data:
                            # ط¥ط²ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ط²ط، ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ (data:application/pdf;base64,)
                            if ',' in scan_data:
                                scan_data = scan_data.split(',', 1)[1]
                                logger.debug("Extracted base64 data after comma")

                            # ظپظƒ طھط±ظ…ظٹط² ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
                            try:
                                file_data = base64.b64decode(scan_data)
                                logger.debug(f"Successfully decoded base64 data, size: {len(file_data)} bytes")
                            except Exception as e:
                                logger.error(f"Error decoding base64 data: {str(e)}")
                                raise ValueError(f"Invalid base64 data: {str(e)}")
                        else:
                            logger.error(f"Invalid scan_data: {type(scan_data)}")
                            raise ValueError("Invalid scan data format")

                        # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
                        file_name = f"scan_{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}.pdf"
                        document.file.save(file_name, ContentFile(file_data), save=False)
                        document.save()
                        logger.debug(f"Document saved with ID: {document.pk}, file path: {document.file.path}")

                        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                        DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                            document=document,
                            status=document.status,
                            notes=_('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¹ظ† ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ'),
                            user=request.user
                        )

                        messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
                        return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.pk)
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error processing scan data: {str(e)}")
                        messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ: ') + str(e))
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Error saving document: {str(e)}")
                    messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: ') + str(e))
            else:
                logger.warning(f"Form errors: {form.errors}")
                messages.error(request, _('ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ طھطµط­ظٹط­ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط§ظ„طھط§ظ„ظٹط©: ') + str(form.errors))
        else:
            logger.warning("No scan_data in request.POST")
            messages.error(request, _('ظ„ظ… ظٹطھظ… طھظˆظپظٹط± ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ.'))
    else:
        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط© ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط±ط§ط¨ط· (ط¥ظ† ظˆط¬ط¯طھ)
        association_id = request.GET.get('association')
        initial_data = {}
        if association_id:
            initial_data['association'] = association_id

        form = ScanDocumentForm(initial=initial_data)

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظ„ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨
    document_types = DocumentType.objects.all()

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط±ظ…ط² CSRF ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط³ظٹط§ظ‚
    from django.middleware.csrf import get_token
    csrf_token = get_token(request)
    logger.debug(f"CSRF Token generated for scan_document")

    return render(request, 'documents/scan_document.html', {
        'form': form,
        'document_types': document_types,
        'csrf_token': csrf_token,
        'title': _('ظ…ط³ط­ ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚')
    })

@login_required
def document_detail(request, pk):
    """ط¹ط±ط¶ طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document.objects.select_related('association', 'document_type', 'uploaded_by', 'reviewed_by'), pk=pk)

    # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظ„ط¯ظٹظ‡ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©طŒ ظ†ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط©
    can_review = request.user.has_perm('documents.change_document')
    review_form = None
    comment_form = DocumentCommentForm()

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
    # طھط­ط³ظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط£ط¯ط§ط،: ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… select_related ظ„طھظ‚ظ„ظٹظ„ ط¹ط¯ط¯ ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ…ط§طھ
    history = document.history.select_related('user').order_by('-created_at')

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ط§طھ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
    comments = document.comments.select_related('user').order_by('-created_at')

    if can_review:
        review_form = DocumentReviewForm(instance=document, user=request.user)

    return render(request, 'documents/document_detail.html', {
        'document': document,
        'history': history,
        'comments': comments,
        'can_review': can_review,
        'review_form': review_form,
        'comment_form': comment_form,
        'title': document.title
    })

@login_required
def document_update(request, pk):
    """طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document, pk=pk)

    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظ‡ظˆ ظ…ظ† ط±ظپط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط£ظˆ ظ„ط¯ظٹظ‡ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¤ظˆظ„
    if not request.user.is_staff and document.uploaded_by != request.user:
        messages.error(request, _('ظ„ظٹط³ ظ„ط¯ظٹظƒ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط© ظ„طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ ظ‡ط°ظ‡ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©.'))
        return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.id)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            # ط§ط­طھظپط¸ ط¨ظ…ط³ط§ط± ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ظپظٹ ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط­ط§ط¬ط© ظ„ط­ط°ظپظ‡
            old_file_path = None
            if document.file and hasattr(document.file, 'path') and os.path.exists(document.file.path):
                old_file_path = document.file.path

            form = DocumentForm(request.POST, request.FILES, instance=document, user=request.user)
            if form.is_valid():
                # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط­ط¯ط«ط©
                updated_document = form.save(commit=False)

                # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ…ط¹ظ„ظˆظ…ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھط­ظ…ظٹظ„ ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
                if 'file' in request.FILES:
                    file = request.FILES['file']
                    updated_document.file_size = file.size
                    updated_document.file_type = file.content_type

                    # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھط­ظ…ظٹظ„ ظ…ظ„ظپ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ظˆظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط¯ظٹظ… ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
                    if old_file_path and os.path.exists(old_file_path):
                        try:
                            os.remove(old_file_path)
                        except Exception as e:
                            print(f"Error deleting old file: {str(e)}")

                updated_document.save()

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                    document=updated_document,
                    status=updated_document.status,
                    notes=_('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                    user=request.user
                )

                messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
                return redirect('documents:detail', pk=updated_document.pk)
            else:
                # ط¹ط±ط¶ ط£ط®ط·ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬
                for field, errors in form.errors.items():
                    for error in errors:
                        messages.error(request, f"{field}: {error}")
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: {}').format(str(e)))
    else:
        form = DocumentForm(instance=document, user=request.user)

    return render(request, 'documents/document_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'document': document,
        'title': _('طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
    })

@login_required
def document_delete(request, pk):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ظ…ط¹ ط§ظ„طھط£ظƒظٹط¯"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document, pk=pk)

    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظ‡ظˆ ظ…ظ† ط±ظپط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط£ظˆ ظ„ط¯ظٹظ‡ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¤ظˆظ„
    if not request.user.is_staff and document.uploaded_by != request.user:
        messages.error(request, _('ظ„ظٹط³ ظ„ط¯ظٹظƒ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط© ظ„ط­ط°ظپ ظ‡ط°ظ‡ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©.'))
        return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.id)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ظ„ط¹ط±ط¶ظ‡ ظپظٹ ط±ط³ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ†ط¬ط§ط­
        document_title = document.title

        try:
            # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ظپط¹ظ„ظٹ ظ…ظ† ظ†ط¸ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
            if document.file and hasattr(document.file, 'path') and os.path.exists(document.file.path):
                os.remove(document.file.path)

            # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ظ…ظ† ظ‚ط§ط¹ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
            document.delete()

            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
            Notification.objects.create(
                title=_('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                message=_('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: {}').format(document_title),
                notification_type='system',
                user=request.user,
                association=document.association
            )

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© "{}" ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.').format(document_title))
            return redirect('documents:list')
        except Exception as e:
            messages.error(request, _('ط­ط¯ط« ط®ط·ط£ ط£ط«ظ†ط§ط، ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©: {}').format(str(e)))
            return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.id)

    # ط¹ط±ط¶ طµظپط­ط© ط§ظ„طھط£ظƒظٹط¯
    return render(request, 'documents/document_confirm_delete.html', {
        'document': document,
        'title': _('طھط£ظƒظٹط¯ ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
    })

@login_required
def document_review(request, pk):
    """ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document, pk=pk)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = DocumentReviewForm(request.POST, instance=document, user=request.user)
        if form.is_valid():
            document = form.save(commit=False)
            document.reviewed_by = request.user
            document.review_date = timezone.now()
            document.save()

            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
            DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                document=document,
                status=document.status,
                notes=form.cleaned_data.get('notes', _('طھظ…طھ ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')),
                user=request.user
            )

            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
            Notification.objects.create(
                title=_('طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                message=_('طھظ… طھط؛ظٹظٹط± ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© "{}" ط¥ظ„ظ‰ "{}".').format(
                    document.title, document.get_status_display()
                ),
                notification_type='document_status',
                association=document.association,
                expires_at=timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(days=30)
            )

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ…طھ ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.pk)
    else:
        form = DocumentReviewForm(instance=document, user=request.user)

    return render(request, 'documents/document_review_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'document': document,
        'title': _('ظ…ط±ط§ط¬ط¹ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
    })

@login_required
def document_comment(request, pk):
    """ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document, pk=pk)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
        if not request.POST:
            messages.error(request, _('ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط؛ظٹط± طµط§ظ„ط­ط©'))
            return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.pk)

        form = DocumentCommentForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
        if form.is_valid():
            comment = form.save(commit=False)
            comment.document = document
            comment.user = request.user

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط­طھظˆظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¶ط§ط±
            comment_text = comment.comment
            if comment_text and len(comment_text.strip()) > 0:
                # ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط²ظٹط¯ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ط§طھ ظ‡ظ†ط§ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط­طھظˆظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¶ط§ط±
                comment.save()

            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ط§ظ„طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯
            Notification.objects.create(
                title=_('طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©'),
                message=_('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© "{}" ط¨ظˆط§ط³ط·ط© {}.').format(
                    document.title, request.user.get_full_name() or request.user.username
                ),
                notification_type='document_comment',
                association=document.association,
                expires_at=timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(days=30)
            )

            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط§ظ„طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))

    return redirect('documents:detail', pk=document.pk)

@login_required
def document_history(request, pk):
    """ط¹ط±ط¶ ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©"""
    document = get_object_or_404(Document.objects.select_related('association', 'document_type'), pk=pk)
    history_entries = DocumentHistory.objects.filter(document=document).select_related('user').order_by('-created_at')

    return render(request, 'documents/document_history.html', {
        'document': document,
        'history_entries': history_entries,
        'title': _('ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
    })

@login_required
# طھظ… طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ظ…طھط·ظ„ط¨ط§طھ ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ظ…ط¤ظ‚طھظ‹ط§ ظ„ظ„طھط£ظƒط¯ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط´ظƒظ„ط© ظ„ظٹط³طھ ظپظٹ ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ
# @permission_required('documents.add_document', raise_exception=True)
def api_scan_upload(request):
    """ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ط© API ظ„ط±ظپط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ"""
    logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    logger.debug(f"api_scan_upload: method={request.method}")

    # ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ط±ظ…ط² CSRF ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط³ظٹط§ظ‚
    from django.middleware.csrf import get_token
    get_token(request)  # ظ„ظ„طھط£ظƒط¯ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط±ظ…ط² CSRF ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ„ط³ط©
    logger.debug(f"CSRF Token generated for API")

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            logger.debug(f"Request headers: {request.headers}")

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ…ط­طھظˆظ‰ ط§ظ„ط·ظ„ط¨
            if not request.body:
                logger.error("Empty request body")
                return JsonResponse({'error': 'Empty request body'}, status=400)

            # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ظƒظ€ JSON
            try:
                data = json.loads(request.body)
                logger.debug(f"Parsed JSON data: {data.keys()}")
            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                logger.error(f"JSON decode error: {str(e)}")

                # ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ظƒظ€ form-data ط¥ط°ط§ ظپط´ظ„ ط§ظ„طھط­ظ„ظٹظ„ ظƒظ€ JSON
                if request.POST or request.FILES:
                    data = {}
                    for key in request.POST:
                        data[key] = request.POST.get(key)

                    if 'file' in request.FILES:
                        file_obj = request.FILES['file']
                        logger.debug(f"File from form-data: {file_obj.name}, size={file_obj.size}")
                        # ظ‚ط±ط§ط،ط© ظ…ط­طھظˆظ‰ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ظˆطھط­ظˆظٹظ„ظ‡ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ base64
                        import base64
                        file_content = file_obj.read()
                        data['scan_data'] = base64.b64encode(file_content).decode('utf-8')

                    logger.debug(f"Extracted form data: {data.keys()}")
                else:
                    return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid JSON format and no form data found'}, status=400)

            # ط§ط³طھط®ط±ط§ط¬ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط·ظ„ظˆط¨ط©
            scan_data = data.get('scan_data')
            title = data.get('title')
            association_id = data.get('association_id')
            description = data.get('description', '')

            # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ط²ط§ظ…ظٹط©
            if not scan_data:
                logger.error("No scan data provided")
                return JsonResponse({'error': 'No scan data provided'}, status=400)

            if not title:
                logger.error("Title is required")
                return JsonResponse({'error': 'Title is required'}, status=400)

            if not association_id:
                logger.error("Association ID is required")
                return JsonResponse({'error': 'Association ID is required'}, status=400)

            # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©
            document = Document(
                title=title,
                association_id=association_id,
                description=description,
                uploaded_by=request.user,
                is_scanned=True,
                scan_date=timezone.now(),
                status='PENDING'
            )

            # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© طھظ„ظ‚ط§ط¦ظٹظ‹ط§ (ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ)
            default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.first()  # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط£ظˆظ„ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
            if not default_document_type:
                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
                default_document_type = DocumentType.objects.create(
                    name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                    description=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظپطھط±ط§ط¶ظٹ'),
                    required=False,
                    processing_deadline=30
                )

            document.document_type = default_document_type

            # طھط­ظˆظٹظ„ ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ base64 ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ظ„ظپ
            try:
                # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† طھظ†ط³ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
                if isinstance(scan_data, str):
                    # ط¥ط²ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ط²ط، ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ (data:application/pdf;base64,)
                    if ',' in scan_data:
                        scan_data = scan_data.split(',', 1)[1]

                    # ظپظƒ طھط±ظ…ظٹط² ط§ظ„ط¨ظٹط§ظ†ط§طھ
                    try:
                        file_data = base64.b64decode(scan_data)
                        logger.debug(f"Successfully decoded base64 data, size: {len(file_data)} bytes")
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"Error decoding base64 data: {str(e)}")
                        return JsonResponse({'error': f'Invalid base64 data: {str(e)}'}, status=400)
                else:
                    logger.error(f"scan_data is not a string but {type(scan_data)}")
                    return JsonResponse({'error': 'scan_data must be a base64 encoded string'}, status=400)

                # ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ
                file_name = f"scan_{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}.pdf"
                document.file.save(file_name, ContentFile(file_data), save=False)
                document.save()

                logger.debug(f"Document saved with ID: {document.pk}, file path: {document.file.path}")

                # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط³ط¬ظ„ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©
                DocumentHistory.objects.create(
                    document=document,
                    status=document.status,
                    notes=_('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¹ظ† ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ (API)'),
                    user=request.user
                )

                return JsonResponse({
                    'success': True,
                    'document_id': document.pk,
                    'message': _('طھظ… ط±ظپط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط­ ط§ظ„ط¶ظˆط¦ظٹ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­')
                })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error processing file data: {str(e)}")
                return JsonResponse({'error': f'Error processing file data: {str(e)}'}, status=500)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Unexpected error: {str(e)}")
            return JsonResponse({'error': str(e)}, status=500)

    return JsonResponse({'error': 'Method not allowed'}, status=405)

@login_required
def api_check_deadlines(_):
    """ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ط© API ظ„ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ظˆط§ط¹ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚"""
    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ طھظ… ط¨ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ط²ط®ط±ظپ @permission_required
    logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    logger.debug("Running api_check_deadlines")

    try:
        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ„ظ‡ط§ ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©
        docs_with_deadline = Document.objects.filter(
            status='PENDING',
            processing_deadline__isnull=False
        )

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ„ظٹط³ ظ„ظ‡ط§ ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظˆظ„ظƒظ† ظ„ظ‡ط§ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ
        docs_with_type_deadline = Document.objects.filter(
            status='PENDING',
            processing_deadline=None,
            document_type__processing_deadline__isnull=False
        )

        # ظپط­طµ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ„ظ‡ط§ ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ
        for doc in docs_with_type_deadline:
            try:
                doc.check_deadline()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error checking deadline for document {doc.id}: {str(e)}")

        # ظپط­طµ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„طھظٹ ظ„ظ‡ط§ ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط©
        for doc in docs_with_deadline:
            try:
                doc.check_deadline()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error checking deadline for document {doc.id}: {str(e)}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error in api_check_deadlines: {str(e)}")
        return JsonResponse({'error': str(e)}, status=500)

    return JsonResponse({
        'success': True,
        'checked_documents': docs_with_deadline.count() + docs_with_type_deadline.count()
    })

@login_required
def document_type_list(request):
    """ط¹ط±ط¶ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط£ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚"""

    return render(request, 'documents/document_type_list.html', {'document_types': document_types})

@login_required
def document_type_create(request):
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = DocumentTypeForm(request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            return redirect('documents:document_type_list')
    else:
        form = DocumentTypeForm()

    return render(request, 'documents/document_type_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'title': _('ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظ†ظˆط¹ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯')
    })

@login_required
def document_type_update(request, pk):
    document_type = get_object_or_404(DocumentType, pk=pk)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = DocumentTypeForm(request.POST, instance=document_type)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
            return redirect('documents:document_type_list')
    else:
        form = DocumentTypeForm(instance=document_type)

    return render(request, 'documents/document_type_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'document_type': document_type,
        'title': _('طھط¹ط¯ظٹظ„ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')
    })

@login_required
def statistics_view(request):
    """ط¹ط±ط¶ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظˆط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ"""
    from .utils import get_document_statistics, get_association_statistics

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…ط©
    document_stats = get_document_statistics()

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ
    association_stats = get_association_statistics()

    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظˆط«ط§ط¦ظ‚ ظ„ظƒظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
    association_document_stats = []
    associations = Association.objects.filter(is_deleted=False).order_by('name')

    for association in associations:
        stats = get_document_statistics(association)
        association_document_stats.append({
            'association': association,
            'stats': stats
        })

    return render(request, 'documents/statistics.html', {
        'document_stats': document_stats,
        'association_stats': association_stats,
        'association_document_stats': association_document_stats,
        'title': _('ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…')
    })

class NotificationForm(forms.ModelForm):

        fields = ['association', 'notification_type', 'title', 'message', 'scheduled_at']
        widgets = {
            'association': forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-select'}),
            'notification_type': forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-select'}),
            'title': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'message': forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 4, 'class': 'form-control'}),
            'scheduled_at': forms.DateTimeInput(attrs={'type': 'datetime-local', 'class': 'form-control'}),
        }

from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.utils import timezone

class Notification(models.Model):
    NOTIFICATION_TYPES = (
        ('tenure_expiry', _('ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©')),
        ('tenure_expiry_soon', _('ط§ظ‚طھط±ط§ط¨ ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©')),
        ('tenure_expiry_very_soon', _('ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ظ‚ط±ظٹط¨ط§ظ‹')),
        ('tenure_expired', _('ط§ظ†طھظ‡طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©')),
        ('warning', _('ط¥ط¹ط°ط§ط±')),
        ('suspension', _('طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚')),
        ('document_status', _('ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')),
        ('document_deadline', _('ظ…ظˆط¹ط¯ ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¦ظٹ ظ„ظ„ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')),
        ('document_comment', _('طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ‚ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظˆط«ظٹظ‚ط©')),
        ('system', _('ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…')),
    )

    title = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±'))
    message = models.TextField(verbose_name=_('ظ†طµ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±'))
    notification_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=NOTIFICATION_TYPES, default='system', verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±'))

    # ط§ظ„ط¹ظ„ط§ظ‚ط§طھ
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='notifications', null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'))
    association = models.ForeignKey('associations.Association', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, related_name='notifications', verbose_name=_('ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'))

    # ط­ظ‚ظˆظ„ ظ„ظ„ط¹ظ„ط§ظ‚ط© ط¨ط§ظ„ظƒط§ط¦ظ†ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط£ط®ط±ظ‰
    related_object_id = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ…ط¹ط±ظپ ط§ظ„ظƒط§ط¦ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·'))
    related_object_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظƒط§ط¦ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط±طھط¨ط·'))

    # ط­ظ‚ظˆظ„ ط§ظ„طھظˆظ‚ظٹطھ
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط،'))
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط،'))
    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('طھظ…طھ ط§ظ„ظ‚ط±ط§ط،ط©'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±')
        verbose_name_plural = _('ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    def is_expired(self):
        """ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ…ط§ ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ…ظ†طھظ‡ظٹ ط§ظ„طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط©"""
        if self.expires_at:
            return timezone.now() > self.expires_at
        return False

    def mark_as_read(self):
        """طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ… ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظƒظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،"""
        self.is_read = True
        self.save()

    @classmethod
    def create_tenure_expiry_notification(cls, association, notification_type='tenure_expiry'):
        """ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©"""
        if not association.tenure_end_date:
            return None

        # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
        days_remaining = (association.tenure_end_date - timezone.now().date()).days

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ…ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¨ط§ظ„ط³ظ†ظˆط§طھ
        tenure_duration = association.get_tenure_duration()

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظˆظ†طµ ط§ظ„ط±ط³ط§ظ„ط© ط¨ظ†ط§ط،ظ‹ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط©
        if days_remaining < 0:
            title = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ†طھظ‡طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name}"
            message = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name} (ظ…ط¯طھظ‡ط§ {tenure_duration}) ط§ظ†طھظ‡طھ ظپظٹ طھط§ط±ظٹط® {association.tenure_end_date}. ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط§طھط®ط§ط° ط§ظ„ط¥ط¬ط±ط§ط،ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ„ط§ط²ظ…ط© ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ ظپظˆط±ط§ظ‹."
            notification_type = 'tenure_expired'
        elif days_remaining <= 7:
            title = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡ ط¹ط§ط¬ظ„: ط§ظ‚طھط±ط§ط¨ ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name}"
            message = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡ ط¹ط§ط¬ظ„: ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name} (ظ…ط¯طھظ‡ط§ {tenure_duration}) ط³طھظ†طھظ‡ظٹ ط®ظ„ط§ظ„ {days_remaining} ظٹظˆظ… ظپظ‚ط· (ط¨طھط§ط±ظٹط® {association.tenure_end_date}). ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط§طھط®ط§ط° ط§ظ„ط¥ط¬ط±ط§ط،ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ„ط§ط²ظ…ط© ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ."
            notification_type = 'tenure_expiry_very_soon'
        elif days_remaining <= 14:
            title = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ‚طھط±ط§ط¨ ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name}"
            message = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name} (ظ…ط¯طھظ‡ط§ {tenure_duration}) ط³طھظ†طھظ‡ظٹ ط®ظ„ط§ظ„ {days_remaining} ظٹظˆظ… (ط¨طھط§ط±ظٹط® {association.tenure_end_date}). ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط§طھط®ط§ط° ط§ظ„ط¥ط¬ط±ط§ط،ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ„ط§ط²ظ…ط© ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ."
            notification_type = 'tenure_expiry_soon'
        else:
            title = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name}"
            message = f"طھظ†ط¨ظٹظ‡: ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط§ظ„ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ظٹط© ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© {association.name} (ظ…ط¯طھظ‡ط§ {tenure_duration}) ط³طھظ†طھظ‡ظٹ ظپظٹ طھط§ط±ظٹط® {association.tenure_end_date} (ط®ظ„ط§ظ„ {days_remaining} ظٹظˆظ…). ظٹط±ط¬ظ‰ ط§طھط®ط§ط° ط§ظ„ط¥ط¬ط±ط§ط،ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ„ط§ط²ظ…ط© ظ„طھط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ظƒطھط¨ ط§ظ„طھظ†ظپظٹط°ظٹ."

        # طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± (ط¨ط¹ط¯ 7 ط£ظٹط§ظ…)
        expires_at = timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(days=7)

        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±
        notification = cls.objects.create(
            title=title,
            message=message,
            notification_type=notification_type,
            association=association,
            expires_at=expires_at
        )

        return notification

from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib import messages
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.db.models import Q
from .models import Notification
from apps.associations.models import Association
from .utils import check_tenure_expiry_notifications, get_active_notifications

@login_required
def notification_list(request):
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ ظˆط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…ط©
    notifications = Notification.objects.filter(
        Q(user=request.user) | Q(user__isnull=True)
    )

    # طھطµظپظٹط© ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط© ط¥ط°ط§ طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ظ‡ط§
    association_id = request.GET.get('association')
    association = None

    if association_id:
        try:
            association = Association.objects.get(pk=association_id)
            notifications = notifications.filter(association_id=association_id)
        except (Association.DoesNotExist, ValueError):
            pass

    # طھط±طھظٹط¨ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط­ط³ط¨ طھط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، (ط§ظ„ط£ط­ط¯ط« ط£ظˆظ„ط§ظ‹)
    notifications = notifications.order_by('-created_at')

    return render(request, 'notifications/notification_list.html', {
        'notifications': notifications,
        'association': association
    })

@login_required
def notification_detail(request, pk):
    # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط³ظˆط§ط، ظƒط§ظ† ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ ط£ظˆ ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¹ط§ظ…
    notification = get_object_or_404(Notification, pk=pk)

    # طھط¹ظ„ظٹظ… ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظƒظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،
    if not notification.is_read:
        notification.mark_as_read()

    return render(request, 'notifications/notification_detail.html', {'notification': notification})

@login_required
def mark_as_read(request, pk):
    notification = get_object_or_404(Notification, pk=pk)
    notification.mark_as_read()
    messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظƒظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،.'))
    return redirect('notifications:list')

@login_required
def check_notifications(request):
    """ظپط­طµ ظˆط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ†طھظ‡ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©"""
    notifications = check_tenure_expiry_notifications()
    if notifications:
        messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، {} ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯.').format(len(notifications)))
    else:
        messages.info(request, _('ظ„ط§ طھظˆط¬ط¯ ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط¬ط¯ظٹط¯ط©.'))
    return redirect('notifications:list')

@login_required
def get_notifications_ajax(request):
    """ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ†ط´ط·ط© ط¨طµظٹط؛ط© JSON"""
    notifications = get_active_notifications()
    data = [{
        'id': notification.id,
        'title': notification.title,
        'message': notification.message,
        'type': notification.notification_type,
        'created_at': notification.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M'),
    } for notification in notifications[:5]]  # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط¢ط®ط± 5 ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ظپظ‚ط·

    return JsonResponse({
        'notifications': data,
        'count': len(notifications)
    })

@login_required
def create_tenure_notification(request, association_id):
    """ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ظ…ظ† طµظپط­ط© طھظپط§طµظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©"""
    association = get_object_or_404(Association, pk=association_id)

    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ طھظˆط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
    if not association.tenure_start_date or not association.tenure_end_date:
        messages.error(request, _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ„ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ظ„ط£ظ† طھظˆط§ط±ظٹط® ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط؛ظٹط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯ط©.'))
        return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association_id)

    # ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط© ظ„ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©
    days_remaining = (association.tenure_end_date - timezone.now().date()).days

    # طھط­ط¯ظٹط¯ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ظ†ط§ط،ظ‹ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ط§ظ„ط£ظٹط§ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…طھط¨ظ‚ظٹط©
    notification_type = 'tenure_expiry'
    if days_remaining < 0:
        notification_type = 'tenure_expired'
    elif days_remaining <= 7:
        notification_type = 'tenure_expiry_very_soon'
    elif days_remaining <= 14:
        notification_type = 'tenure_expiry_soon'

    # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±
    notification = Notification.create_tenure_expiry_notification(association, notification_type)

    if notification:
        messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط© ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
    else:
        messages.error(request, _('ظپط´ظ„ ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ط§ظ„ط¹ظ‡ط¯ط©.'))

    return redirect('associations:detail', pk=association_id)

@login_required
def delete_notification(request, pk):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ…ط­ط¯ط¯"""
    notification = get_object_or_404(Notification, pk=pk)

    # ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±
    notification.delete()

    messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.'))
    return redirect('notifications:list')

@login_required
def delete_all_read_notifications(request):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط©"""
    # ط­ط°ظپ ط¬ظ…ظٹط¹ ط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط© ظ„ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط§ظ„ط­ط§ظ„ظٹ ظˆط§ظ„ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط©
    deleted_count = Notification.objects.filter(
        Q(user=request.user) | Q(user__isnull=True),
        is_read=True
    ).delete()[0]

    if deleted_count > 0:
        messages.success(request, _('طھظ… ط­ط°ظپ {} ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط± ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط، ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­.').format(deleted_count))
    else:
        messages.info(request, _('ظ„ط§ طھظˆط¬ط¯ ط¥ط´ط¹ط§ط±ط§طھ ظ…ظ‚ط±ظˆط،ط© ظ„ظ„ط­ط°ظپ.'))

    return redirect('notifications:list')

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.admin import UserAdmin as BaseUserAdmin
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from .models import UserProfile

class UserProfileInline(admin.StackedInline):
    model = UserProfile
    can_delete = False
    verbose_name_plural = 'ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµظٹ'

class UserAdmin(BaseUserAdmin):
    inlines = (UserProfileInline,)

class UserProfileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('user', 'user_type', 'phone', 'position')
    list_filter = ('user_type',)
    search_fields = ('user__username', 'user__email', 'phone')

# Re-register UserAdmin
admin.site.unregister(User)
admin.site.register(User, UserAdmin)
admin.site.register(UserProfile, UserProfileAdmin)

from django import forms
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.contrib.auth.forms import UserCreationForm, AuthenticationForm
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _

class CustomUserCreationForm(UserCreationForm):
    email = forms.EmailField(required=True)
    
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ['username', 'email', 'password1', 'password2']
        labels = {
            'username': _('ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'),
            'email': _('ط§ظ„ط¨ط±ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ظƒطھط±ظˆظ†ظٹ'),
        }
    
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.fields['password1'].label = _('ظƒظ„ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ظˆط±')
        self.fields['password2'].label = _('طھط£ظƒظٹط¯ ظƒظ„ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط±ظˆط±')

class UserProfileForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ['username', 'email', 'first_name', 'last_name']
        labels = {
            'username': _('ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'),
            'email': _('ط§ظ„ط¨ط±ظٹط¯ ط§ظ„ط¥ظ„ظƒطھط±ظˆظ†ظٹ'),
            'first_name': _('ط§ظ„ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ط£ظˆظ„'),
            'last_name': _('ط§ط³ظ… ط§ظ„ط¹ط§ط¦ظ„ط©'),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):

from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db.models.signals import post_save
from django.dispatch import receiver
from django.utils import timezone

class UserProfile(models.Model):
    USER_TYPE_CHOICES = (
        ('ADMIN', 'ظ…ط¯ظٹط± ط§ظ„ظ†ط¸ط§ظ…'),
        ('MUNICIPAL_STAFF', 'ظ…ظˆط¸ظپ ط¨ظ„ط¯ظٹط©'),
        ('ASSOCIATION_REPRESENTATIVE', 'ظ…ظ…ط«ظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©'),
        ('STAFF', 'ظ…ظˆط¸ظپ'),
        ('USER', 'ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'),
    )

    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='profile')
    user_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=USER_TYPE_CHOICES, default='USER', verbose_name='ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…')
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name='ط±ظ‚ظ… ط§ظ„ظ‡ط§طھظپ')
    position = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name='ط§ظ„ظ…ظ†طµط¨')
    association = models.ForeignKey('associations.Association', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©', related_name='representatives')

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'
        verbose_name_plural = 'ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ†'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - {self.get_user_type_display()}"

@receiver(post_save, sender=User)
def create_user_profile(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    if created:
        UserProfile.objects.create(user=instance)

@receiver(post_save, sender=User)
def save_user_profile(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    # ظ„ط§ ظ†ظ‚ظˆظ… ط¨ط­ظپط¸ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµظٹ ط¹ظ†ط¯ طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط­ظ‚ظ„ last_login
    # ظ‡ط°ط§ ظٹط­ط¯ط« ط¹ظ†ط¯ طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¯ط®ظˆظ„ ظˆظٹظ…ظƒظ† ط£ظ† ظٹط³ط¨ط¨ ظ…ط´ط§ظƒظ„
    update_fields = kwargs.get('update_fields')
    if update_fields is not None and update_fields == frozenset(['last_login']):
        return

    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ…ظ„ظپ ط´ط®طµظٹ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ظ…ط­ط§ظˆظ„ط© ط­ظپط¸ظ‡
    try:
        # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµظٹ ط¨ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط·ط±ظٹظ‚ط© ط£ظƒط«ط± ط£ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‹ط§
        profile = UserProfile.objects.get(user=instance)
        profile.save()
    except UserProfile.DoesNotExist:
        # ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ظ…ظ„ظپ ط´ط®طµظٹ ط¥ط°ط§ ظ„ظ… ظٹظƒظ† ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ظ‹ط§
        UserProfile.objects.create(user=instance)

class UserStatus(models.Model):
    """
    ظ†ظ…ظˆط°ط¬ ظ„طھطھط¨ط¹ ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ†
    """
    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='status')
    is_online = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    last_seen = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    last_activity = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    current_page = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'ط­ط§ظ„ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…'
        verbose_name_plural = 'ط­ط§ظ„ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ†'
        
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - {'ظ…طھطµظ„' if self.is_online else 'ط؛ظٹط± ظ…طھطµظ„'}"
    
    def update_activity(self, page=None):
        """
        طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ†ط´ط§ط· ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
        """
        self.last_activity = timezone.now()
        if page:
            self.current_page = page
        self.save()
        
    def set_online(self):
        """
        طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظƒظ…طھطµظ„
        """
        self.is_online = True
        self.last_seen = timezone.now()
        self.save()
        
    def set_offline(self):
        """
        طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظƒط؛ظٹط± ظ…طھطµظ„
        """
        self.is_online = False
        self.last_seen = timezone.now()
        self.save()

from django.views.generic import ListView, DetailView, CreateView, UpdateView
from django.contrib.auth.views import LoginView
from django.contrib.auth.mixins import UserPassesTestMixin
from django.urls import reverse_lazy
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required, permission_required
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from .forms import CustomUserCreationForm, UserProfileForm, CustomAuthenticationForm
from .models import UserProfile
import logging

class CustomLoginView(LoginView):
    template_name = 'users/login.html'
    form_class = CustomAuthenticationForm
    redirect_authenticated_user = True

def register(request):
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = CustomUserCreationForm(request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            username = form.cleaned_data.get('username')
            messages.success(request, _(f'طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط¨ط§ط³ظ… {username}! ظٹظ…ظƒظ†ظƒ ط§ظ„ط¢ظ† طھط³ط¬ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط¯ط®ظˆظ„'))
            return redirect('users:login')
    else:
        form = CustomUserCreationForm()
    return render(request, 'users/register.html', {'form': form})

@login_required
def profile(request):
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = UserProfileForm(request.POST, instance=request.user)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            messages.success(request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپ ط§ظ„ط´ط®طµظٹ ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­!'))
            return redirect('users:profile')
    else:
        form = UserProfileForm(instance=request.user)

    return render(request, 'users/profile.html', {'form': form})

# ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ط§طھ ط¥ط¯ط§ط±ط© ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ†
class AdminRequiredMixin(UserPassesTestMixin):
    def test_func(self):
        return self.request.user.is_authenticated and self.request.user.profile.user_type == 'ADMIN'

class UserListView(AdminRequiredMixin, ListView):
    model = User
    template_name = 'users/admin/user_list.html'
    context_object_name = 'users'
    paginate_by = 10

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        search_query = self.request.GET.get('q', '')
        user_type = self.request.GET.get('user_type', '')

        # ط§ظ„ط¨ط­ط« ظپظٹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ† ط¨ط§ط³طھط®ط¯ط§ظ… ط§ظ„ط§ط³طھط¹ظ„ط§ظ…
        if search_query:
            queryset = queryset.filter(
                username__icontains=search_query
            ) | queryset.filter(
                email__icontains=search_query
            ) | queryset.filter(
                first_name__icontains=search_query
            ) | queryset.filter(
                last_name__icontains=search_query
            )

        # ظپظ„طھط±ط© ط­ط³ط¨ ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
        if user_type:
            queryset = queryset.filter(profile__user_type=user_type)

        return queryset

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context['user_types'] = UserProfile.USER_TYPE_CHOICES
        context['search_query'] = self.request.GET.get('q', '')
        context['selected_user_type'] = self.request.GET.get('user_type', '')
        return context

class UserDetailView(AdminRequiredMixin, DetailView):
    model = User
    template_name = 'users/admin/user_detail.html'
    context_object_name = 'user_obj'

class UserCreateView(AdminRequiredMixin, CreateView):
    model = User
    template_name = 'users/admin/user_form.html'
    form_class = CustomUserCreationForm
    success_url = reverse_lazy('users:admin_user_list')

    def form_valid(self, form):
        response = super().form_valid(form)
        user = form.instance

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ…ظ„ظپ طھط¹ط±ظٹظپ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
        user_type = self.request.POST.get('user_type', 'USER')
        profile = user.profile
        profile.user_type = user_type
        profile.phone = self.request.POST.get('phone', '')
        profile.position = self.request.POST.get('position', '')

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ…ظ…ط«ظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©طŒ طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        if user_type == 'ASSOCIATION_REPRESENTATIVE':
            association_id = self.request.POST.get('association')
            if association_id and association_id.strip():
                from apps.associations.models import Association
                try:
                    association = Association.objects.get(id=association_id)
                    profile.association = association
                except Association.DoesNotExist:
                    pass

        profile.save()

        messages.success(self.request, _('طھظ… ط¥ظ†ط´ط§ط، ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
        return response

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context['user_types'] = UserProfile.USER_TYPE_CHOICES

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ظ„ظ…ظ…ط«ظ„ظٹظ†
        from apps.associations.models import Association
        context['associations'] = Association.objects.all()

        return context

class UserUpdateView(AdminRequiredMixin, UpdateView):
    model = User
    template_name = 'users/admin/user_form.html'
    form_class = UserProfileForm
    success_url = reverse_lazy('users:admin_user_list')

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context['user_types'] = UserProfile.USER_TYPE_CHOICES
        context['current_user_type'] = self.object.profile.user_type

        # ط§ظ„ط­طµظˆظ„ ط¹ظ„ظ‰ ظ‚ط§ط¦ظ…ط© ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط§طھ ظ„ظ„ظ…ظ…ط«ظ„ظٹظ†
        from apps.associations.models import Association
        context['associations'] = Association.objects.all()
        context['current_association'] = self.object.profile.association

        return context

    def form_valid(self, form):
        response = super().form_valid(form)
        user = form.instance

        # طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ظ…ظ„ظپ طھط¹ط±ظٹظپ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…
        user_type = self.request.POST.get('user_type', user.profile.user_type)
        profile = user.profile
        profile.user_type = user_type
        profile.phone = self.request.POST.get('phone', profile.phone)
        profile.position = self.request.POST.get('position', profile.position)

        # ط¥ط°ط§ ظƒط§ظ† ظ…ظ…ط«ظ„ ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©طŒ طھط¹ظٹظٹظ† ط§ظ„ط¬ظ…ط¹ظٹط©
        if user_type == 'ASSOCIATION_REPRESENTATIVE':
            association_id = self.request.POST.get('association')
            if association_id and association_id.strip():
                from apps.associations.models import Association
                try:
                    association = Association.objects.get(id=association_id)
                    profile.association = association
                except Association.DoesNotExist:
                    pass
        else:
            profile.association = None

        profile.save()

        # ظ…ط¹ط§ظ„ط¬ط© طھظپط¹ظٹظ„/طھط¹ط·ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ط­ط³ط§ط¨
        is_active = self.request.POST.get('is_active') == 'on'
        if user.is_active != is_active:
            user.is_active = is_active
            user.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

        messages.success(self.request, _('طھظ… طھط­ط¯ظٹط« ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
        return response

@login_required
@permission_required('auth.delete_user', raise_exception=True)
def deactivate_user(request, pk):
    user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=pk)

    # ظ„ط§ ظٹط³ظ…ط­ ط¨طھط¹ط·ظٹظ„ ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظ†ظپط³ظ‡
    if request.user == user:
        messages.error(request, _('ظ„ط§ ظٹظ…ظƒظ†ظƒ طھط¹ط·ظٹظ„ ط­ط³ط§ط¨ظƒ ط§ظ„ط®ط§طµ'))
        return redirect('users:admin_user_detail', pk=pk)

    user.is_active = False
    user.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

    messages.success(request, _(f'طھظ… طھط¹ط·ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… {user.username} ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
    return redirect('users:admin_user_list')

@login_required
@permission_required('auth.change_user', raise_exception=True)
def activate_user(request, pk):
    user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=pk)
    user.is_active = True
    user.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

    messages.success(request, _(f'طھظ… طھظپط¹ظٹظ„ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… {user.username} ط¨ظ†ط¬ط§ط­'))
    return redirect('users:admin_user_list')

@login_required
def admin_dashboard(request):
    # ط§ظ„طھط­ظ‚ظ‚ ظ…ظ† ط£ظ† ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ… ظ„ط¯ظٹظ‡ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³ط¤ظˆظ„
    if not request.user.profile.user_type == 'ADMIN':
        messages.error(request, _('ظ„ظٹط³ ظ„ط¯ظٹظƒ طµظ„ط§ط­ظٹط© ط§ظ„ظˆطµظˆظ„ ط¥ظ„ظ‰ ظ„ظˆط­ط© ط§ظ„طھط­ظƒظ…'))
        return redirect('home')

    # ط¥ط­طµط§ط¦ظٹط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ط³طھط®ط¯ظ…ظٹظ†
    total_users = User.objects.count()
    active_users = User.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
    admin_users = User.objects.filter(profile__user_type='ADMIN').count()
    municipal_staff = User.objects.filter(profile__user_type='MUNICIPAL_STAFF').count()
    association_reps = User.objects.filter(profile__user_type='ASSOCIATION_REPRESENTATIVE').count()

    context = {
        'total_users': total_users,
        'active_users': active_users,
        'admin_users': admin_users,
        'municipal_staff': municipal_staff,
        'association_reps': association_reps,
        'recent_users': User.objects.order_by('-date_joined')[:5]
    }

    return render(request, 'users/admin/dashboard.html', context)

from django.contrib import admin
from django.urls import path, include
from django.views.generic import TemplateView
from django.conf import settings
from django.conf.urls.static import static
from apps.documents.views import csrf_failure
from apps.core.views import handler404, handler500  # Import custom error handlers

urlpatterns = [
    path('admin/', admin.site.urls),
    path('', TemplateView.as_view(template_name='home.html'), name='home'),
    path('users/', include('apps.users.urls')),
    path('associations/', include('apps.associations.urls', namespace='associations')),
    path('documents/', include('apps.documents.urls')),
    path('notifications/', include('apps.notifications.urls')),
    path('csrf-error/', csrf_failure, name='csrf_failure'),
]

# ط¥ط¶ط§ظپط© ظ…ط³ط§ط±ط§طھ ط§ظ„ظ…ظ„ظپط§طھ ط§ظ„ط«ط§ط¨طھط© ظˆط§ظ„ظˆط³ط§ط¦ط· ظپظٹ ط¨ظٹط¦ط© ط§ظ„طھط·ظˆظٹط±

    urlpatterns += static(settings.MEDIA_URL, document_root=settings.MEDIA_ROOT)
